- **Freeplay inst preview, threaded.** Yarn loads the selected song's Inst on a background `Thread`+`Mutex` (`FreeplayState.hx:139-171, #if target.threaded`) so browsing never hitches. The feature itself is superseded by Phoenix's `music.MusicPlayer` preview (with UI, volume and icon bounce). The threading trick is noted but not recommended as-is: OpenFL `Sound` loading off the main thread is not safe on all targets (notably Android/web), and Phoenix's low-memory profiles (§18) already govern Freeplay load cost. No entry.
- **Always-on `global` mod.** Yarn's `mods/global` folder is always enabled and loads first. Phoenix covers the intent differently and more flexibly via `pack.runsGlobally` global mods (`backend/Mods.hx:54-63` `pushGlobalMods`). Nothing to port; the Polymod importer in §29.1 should map a Polymod `global` folder onto that same mechanism.
- **Two-player keyboard scheme.** Yarn `Controls` has a `KeyboardScheme.Duo(first)` and `PlayerSettings` keeps (commented-out) `player2` scaffolding, but no gameplay ever reads a second player — it is dead code upstream too. Phoenix's real co-op gap is already §12.4; Yarn adds nothing to it.
- **Data-file formats.** `freeplayList.txt` (`Song:Week:IconChar:0xColor`), `freeplayColors.txt`, `creditsList.txt` (`name::desc`), `characterList.txt`, per-song dialogue `.txt` auto-detection: all are simpler-but-weaker dialects of things Phoenix already loads from WeekData/song/character JSON with colours, icons, links and script hooks. Per §12.8 (do not drop Psych/JS JSON compatibility) these stay importer-free; the *only* dialect importer worth having is the character one in §29.1, because characters are self-contained and editor-produced.

### 29.3. Do not port

- **Hardcoded stages/characters by name.** Yarn builds every stage with a `switch (SONG.song)` / `switch (curCharacter)` in `PlayState.create`/`Character.new` (with `SONG.stage` commented out of `SwagSong`), falling back to JSON only for unknown characters. That is a regression relative to Phoenix's stage/character JSON + stage scripts; only the generic-loader dialect (§29.1) is worth importing.
- **Yarn's A–B loop implementation** (`deepCopyNotes`, score zeroing, `#if debug` timeScale comparison bug) — port the feature (§29.1) on Phoenix's seek machinery, never this code.
- **`ColorsMenu` note-colour editor.** A single hue-rotation value per lane (`Note.arrowColors: [1,1,1,1]` through a `ColorSwap` HSV shader), scrubbed with UI keys — and it is never saved anywhere, so colours reset every launch. Phoenix's per-lane RGB + quant palettes (`arrowRGB`, `arrowRGBPixel`, `quantRGB`, Notes options) are strictly better. Do not port the menu or the single-hue model.
- **The options-page framework itself** (`ui/Page`, `ui/OptionsState`, `ui/Prompt`, AtlasText bitmap-font menus, `MenuTypedList` grid navigation). A legitimate alternative UI architecture, but Phoenix's `BaseOptionsMenu`/`Option` system carries far more settings, search and platform gating; porting the framework would be a rewrite for no user-visible gain.
- **Vendored-but-unused code:** `FlxRuntimeShader` (MasterEric's runtime-shader wrapper — Phoenix's `ErrorHandledRuntimeShader` + script shader system covers it, with error handling Yarn lacks), `DemonBlur`, `BeatBattle.hx` (empty file), the `haxe.zip.Writer` import in the chart editor (imported, never used; chart save is a plain `FileReference` JSON download — Phoenix's editor save/autosave is a superset).
- **Hardcoded version plumbing:** `OutdatedSubState` compares against a literal `v0.2.8` string and links to the base game's itch.io page; Phoenix's `UpdateChecker`/`OutdatedState` already do this properly. (Yarn's own version strings disagree with each other — `0.2.8` in `Project.xml`, `0.1.0-alpha` in the main menu — which is why this stays out.)
- **Platform wrappers with no engine behind them:** `art/build-lime-SWITCH.bat` and the itch/NG build `.bat`s are lime command aliases, not Switch/web platform support; Phoenix's platform work (§3, §11) is the real thing. Do not list Switch as a Yarn feature.

---

## 30. FNF: DOORS PUBLIC REUSE REVIEW (2026-11-21)

Source: [Leetram519/FNF-Doors-Public](https://github.com/Leetram519/FNF-Doors-Public), public `HEAD` **`19cb18a068fb32660eaa7cda55e2ddef576bcfc0`** (2025-08-31). Reviewed its README feature claims and source tree against Phoenix's current tree, including the story/run classes, `MechanicsManager`, `ClientPrefs`/options, `FunkinLua` registrations, online classes, and modchart editor. This is a feature-gap review, not a code port. The public repo has **no root LICENSE file at that revision**; do not copy its implementation, assets, or bundled third-party code until each relevant license and attribution requirement is established. Phoenix's own license/credit rules above still apply.

**Scope filter:** keep reusable engine systems and player-configurable features only. Exclude the Doors mod's songs/weeks/stages/characters, rooms and entity encounters, named items and their effects, story text/art, Doors-specific modifiers/unlock goals, branding, and other game-specific material. Do not turn this section into a request to reproduce Doors itself.

### 30.1 Reusable systems Phoenix does not currently have

- **[P1] Data-driven, multi-step story-run framework (not another week menu).** Doors has a run/room model (`backend/storymode/DoorsRun.hx`, `StoryRoom.hx`), story metadata, state transitions and persistence around a sequence of playable steps. Phoenix has Psych-style week/story selection and result aggregation (`states/StoryMenuState.hx`, `states/ResultsData.hx`), but no general run object that owns a branching/linear sequence, per-run state and continuation between arbitrary mod-defined steps. Add a schema-driven campaign/run contract with stable step IDs, choices/branch transitions, run-local data, save/resume and completion/cancel hooks; allow a mod to supply its own steps without hardcoding them in Phoenix. Reuse §13's profiles/migration and §6's path/schema validation. **Do not port** Doors floors, room layouts, encounters, story events, or its specific progression rules. Effort: L.
- **[P1] Generic item/inventory service for mods.** Doors has an inventory data model (`InventoryManager.ItemData`) and add/remove/lookup/slot handling (`backend/storymode/InventoryManager.hx`); Phoenix has no comparable run-scoped inventory. Add a content-neutral item definition and inventory service with stable IDs, capacity/slot policy, stack/unique rules, optional durability, context restrictions, purchase/use/remove callbacks, serialization, and script access. Item effects and UI should be supplied by mods, not compiled into the engine. Expose consistent Lua/Python/HScript operations only after the native model and validation exist. Keep this optional so ordinary song mods pay no memory or startup cost. Effort: M–L.
- **[P2] Lifecycle-managed mechanic modules.** Doors factors gameplay logic into a `backend.MechanicsManager` base with create/createPost, update/updatePost, note-hit/miss/spawn, beat/step, event, countdown/start/end-song, and death hooks (`backend/MechanicsManager.hx`); Phoenix currently relies on PlayState helpers/stages and script callbacks rather than an equivalent owned mechanic registry. Add a lightweight, deterministic register/unregister manager with explicit ordering, per-song lifetime, safe teardown, error isolation, and lifecycle hooks. Provide script adapters so a mechanic can be implemented in Lua, Python, or HScript without placing new behavior in `PlayState`. This is architecture/API only: none of Doors' named mechanics or effects are included. Effort: M.
- **[P2] Metadata-driven gameplay/run modifier framework (not another note modchart).** Doors has modifier definitions, categories, selection/filtering, per-run selected values and score/price multipliers (`ModifierManager.hx`, `substates/FreeplayModifierSelectSubState.hx`, `backend/storymode/DoorsRun.hx`). Phoenix's `modchart/ModifierRegistry` changes note presentation/position; it is not a registry for player-selected gameplay rules attached to a song or campaign run. If Phoenix wants this capability, define a mod-provided modifier contract (stable ID, description/category, applicability, defaults/config, apply/remove hooks, score eligibility/multiplier policy) plus a generic selector and run-save integration. Keep anti-cheat/scoring semantics explicit and avoid trusting client-provided multipliers for online scores. Do **not** port Doors' modifier names, effects, unlock requirements, or balancing. Effort: M–L.
- **[P2] Song-bound modchart timeline editor (distinct from the Modchart Lab).** Doors integrates a visual editor that authors beat-timed set/ease events and modifier/submodifier values into a song-associated file (`modcharting/ModchartEditorState.hx`, `ModchartEvent*`, `ModchartFile.hx`). Phoenix already has the native runtime, scripting callbacks, multi-playfield model, and the renderer-free Modchart Lab (`editors/ModchartLabState.hx`; §12.5 and §23.1), but the Lab is a loop/preview and code-export tool, not a song-bound event timeline editor. Consider adding a timeline authoring mode to the existing lab/editor: load/save a versioned, mod-local file; edit set/ease/callback/repeater events; preview against a selected chart; validate modifier names and ranges; and retain script control as an escape hatch. Do not import the Modcharting Tools dependency or its format until its license, compatibility and migration costs are checked. Effort: M–L.
- **[P2] Optional online leaderboard provider interface.** Doors includes a Glasshat account/login and song-score fetch/upload path (`online/Glasshat.hx`, `online/Leaderboards.hx`, `flixel/addons/api/FlxGlasshat.hx`); Phoenix's `backend/Highscore.hx` and achievements are local, with no online-score provider. If online competition is wanted, design a replaceable provider interface and consent-based account/link flow with explicit offline behavior, privacy/data-retention rules, bounded requests, server-side validation/rate limits, and no score upload for botplay/cheated/unverifiable runs. Keep local scores functional without network access. Do **not** hardwire Glasshat, assume its service is available, or copy its client/backend; the public source has no root license and the client has a compile-time `GLASSHAT` gate. Effort: L; security/privacy review required.
- **[P3] Optional animated GIF asset support for mods.** Doors bundles `flxgif/FlxGifSpriteAsync.hx` and a Yagp-based decoder; Phoenix has no animated-GIF sprite/asset loader (its existing animation systems are not GIF decoding). If mods request it, add a lazily decoded, disposable sprite/asset API, bounded by frame count, dimensions and decoded-memory budget, with clear unsupported-target fallback. Do not make GIF decoding or a decoder dependency mandatory for all builds; test low-memory Android and hostile/oversized files first. Doors uses this for a game-specific poster, which is excluded; only the general loader is a candidate. Effort: M.

### 30.2 Script API audit

- **No standalone general Lua callback gap was confirmed.** The string-registered functions in Doors' `FunkinLua.hx` are largely Psych-compatible APIs. The additional-looking names found by a static registration scan (`goToRoom`, `reloadRoom`, `lookUpRoom`, `giveItem`, `motherlode`, `toggleDark`, `getAwardPopup`, `clearAchievements`, etc.) are registered on `FlxG.console` in game states, not as general Lua callbacks; several are debug or Doors-story commands. Phoenix's callbacks are split across `source/psychlua/callbacks/` and already include a broader Lua/Python/HScript surface, including modchart, shader, filter, effect, file, input, hardware and platform APIs. Do not add these console commands as presumed Lua API parity.
- When §30.1's generic systems are implemented, their mod API is the actual reusable gap: define typed, validated contracts for story-run/step state, inventory definitions and operations, and mechanic lifecycle/registration, then mirror supported operations across Lua, Python and HScript. Include documented callbacks, ownership/cleanup rules, and tests/examples. Keep service credentials, filesystem paths, arbitrary code execution and trusted score submission out of those APIs.

### 30.3 Player options worth considering

- **[P2] Health-icon placement (`iconsOnHB`, bool, default off).** Doors offers a preference to place health icons within/over the health bar; Phoenix has health-bar style/transparency and multiple icon styles, but no equivalent placement preference. Add only if it can be made independent of character/stage-specific placement and remains legible at small UI scales. Use the existing health-bar bounds and respect `uiScalePercent`; do not retain Doors' stage/character exceptions. Effort: S.
- **[P3] Note-splash opacity (`splashAlpha`, 0–1, default 0.6).** Doors exposes a separate splash-alpha slider; Phoenix's `objects/NoteSplash.hx` currently fixes alpha at 0.6 and already has splash enable/cap controls. A bounded opacity option is a small, content-neutral extension, but should apply consistently to normal and sustain splashes and compose with `adaptivePerformance`/reduced motion. Effort: S.

### 30.4 Audited and intentionally not added as parity work

- **Existing Phoenix equivalents / supersets:** script callbacks and modcharting are not missing (see §12.1, §12.5 and §30.2); the Lab is present although its goal differs from a timeline editor. Phoenix already has local scores/achievements, a results screen, extensive settings, accessibility/color-contrast controls, translation support, loading progress UI, and a lane-underlay option corresponding to Doors' strumline-background opacity. The basic Doors preferences for scroll direction, middle scroll, opponent strums, camera zoom/follow, combo stacking, note offset/timing windows, framerate, shaders, GPU cache and language therefore do not justify duplicate rows.
- **Not an engine accessibility port:** Doors' whole-game colorblind simulation filter (`shaders/ColorblindFilters.hx`) is not a substitute for Phoenix's note-color palettes, lane glyphs, contrast controls and accessibility work in §16/§22. Do not label a color-vision simulation as an accessibility fix or add it without a separate usability review. Doors' film-grain setting is used by its story-menu presentation, so it is not a general engine option to copy.
- **Not generic feature candidates:** the Doors-specific mode/ending content, all named rooms/entities/items/mechanics/modifiers and their unlock logic; its Glasshat provider implementation; one-off debug console cheats; and game-specific graphics/audio. Likewise, `FlxStrip` is used there as part of its custom sustain/modchart renderer; Phoenix's rendering gap is already tracked under §12.5/§23.1, so do not duplicate it as a second renderer task.

---

## 31. SEIUN ENGINE COMPARISON — `mohong2/FNF-SeiunEngine` (2026-11-21)

**Source:** [mohong2/FNF-SeiunEngine](https://github.com/mohong2/FNF-SeiunEngine) at commit `ddaddd8c` (2026-11-21), forked from Psych Engine 0.6.3. **License:** Apache 2.0 (compatible with Phoenix — port with proper credits to `mo_hong` / SeiunEngine Team, plus upstream attributions in its `NOTICE` for Codename-format conventions and `Funkin-Psych-Online` / `Snirozu` Apache 2.0 online architecture). **Method:** full source audit of SeiunEngine against Phoenix Engine (`443ae01` → `383c761`: code, options, Lua/HScript/Python callbacks, editors, graphics/chart pipeline, online/server stack, and tooling); nothing was built or run.

Engine/mod-specific content (weeks, songs, characters, stages, Mandela Funkin Night leftovers, SeiunEngine branding/wordmarks, and license-incompatible third-party shaders) is **explicitly excluded**. Items that overlap §0–§30 are cross-referenced and scoped to what SeiunEngine adds beyond what is already tracked. Per the porting rule at the top of this file, any player-facing feature ported here must ship enabled by default (unless stated otherwise) with a toggle/option.

### 31.1. Modding & Scripting APIs

- **[P1] Sandboxed Lua `require()` and `import()` module loaders (`script/lua/FunkinLua.hx`).** SeiunEngine gives Lua scripts standard cached `require("sub.mod")` (`package.loaded` semantics, `.` → `/`, `?.lua` + `?/init.lua`, search order: script dir → current mod `lua/` & `scripts/` → global mods → `mods/` → `assets/`, with `..` path-traversal blocking) and uncached `import("lib/utils.lua")` (re-executes on every call, preserves multiple return values). Phoenix currently strips `package.loaded` in `backend/ModSecurity.hx:270` to block unsafe LuaJIT/FFI loads and has no virtual module loader. Expose a sandboxed, mod-rooted `require()`/`import()` that runs every loaded file through `ScriptSecurityScanner` and `ModPathPolicy`. Effort: M.
- **[P1] Mod-defined settings (`options/ModSettingsSubState.hx`, `data/settings.json`, `getModSetting`).** Also flagged in §27.1.1 — SeiunEngine provides an additional clean Apache 2.0 reference (`ClientPrefs.modSettings[mod]` + `getModSetting(saveTag, ?modName)` in Lua and HScript) to un-stub `states/ModsMenuState.hx:19` across Lua, Python, and HScript. Effort: M.
- **[P1] Lua support in `ModState`/`ModSubState` + `pack.json` state/substate redirects (`states/ModState.hx`, `backend/ModConfig.hx`).** Phoenix's `psychlua/StateScripts.hx` only loads HScript (`['hx', 'hscript', 'hxs']`) and only supports explicit `switchCustomState()` / `openModSubState()`. SeiunEngine lets `ModState` / `ModSubState` run **Lua** as well as HScript (`data/states/<name>.lua`, `data/states/<name>/`, `lua/<name>/`), adds `pack.json` `stateReplacements` / `substateReplacements` to redirect built-in states (e.g. `TitleState`, `MainMenuState`) without code edits, and adds `closeModSubState`, `switchToState`, `getStateName`, `getSubStateName` (plus `switchToModState` alias). Extends §0 (Common Modding API) & §12.1; extend to Python as well. Effort: M.
- **[P1] Missing Psych 0.7.3 / 1.0.4 reflection & instance Lua callbacks.** Audited `script/lua/FunkinLua.hx` against all `source/psychlua/callbacks/*.hx` files in Phoenix. Missing in Phoenix:
  - Dynamic object creation & method invocation: `createInstance`, `addInstance`, `instanceArg`, `callMethod`, `callMethodFromClass`, `addToGroup`, and `allowInstances` support in `setProperty*` (note: keep gated by `ModSecurity` class allowlists!).
  - Camera follow/scroll helpers: `getCameraFollowX/Y`, `setCameraFollowPoint`, `addCameraFollowPoint`, `getCameraScrollX/Y`, `setCameraScroll`, `addCameraScroll`.
  - Audio, text, sprite & save helpers: `getSoundPitch` / `setSoundPitch`, `eraseSaveData`, `getColorFromName` / `getColorFromString`, `doesLuaSpriteExist` / `doesLuaTextExist`, `setTextAutoSize` / `setTextHeight`, `loadMultipleFrames`, `removeWiggleEffect`, `getGlobals`, `updateScoreText`, `changeDiscordClientID` / `changeDiscordPresence`, `setModWindowIcon`.
  - Per-note runtime mutators & multikey script APIs: `setNoteTexture`, `setNoteCharAnim`, `setNoteColor`, and `getMania` / `setMania` / `changeMania` (pairs with §25.1).
  - Translation callbacks (Psych 1.0.4): `getLanguage`, `loadLanguage`, `getFileTranslation`, `getTranslationPhrase` (pairs with §12.4 localization).
  - Effort: M.
- **[P1] Automatic Desktop GLSL → Mobile GLES2/3 shader converter (`shaders/MobileShaderConverter.hx`).** Token/AST-level GLSL transpiler that queries actual GL capabilities (`configureFromGL`), rewrites desktop GLSL (`#version`, missing `precision`, numeric type promotion, float/int literal suffixes, unsupported builtins) for Android/iOS GLES2/3 with per-line diagnostics and program cache keys. Phoenix's `shaders/ShaderSource.hx` only expands `#include`/`#pragma include` directives, so desktop mod shaders frequently fail to compile on Android. Major payoff for Phoenix's Android-first identity. Effort: L.
- **[P2] Bidirectional HScript ↔ Lua bridge & function-override API (`script/hscript/LuaApi.hx`, `HScript.hx`).** Lets HScript register, wrap/override (with a callable `original` proxy to avoid infinite recursion on engine callbacks), rename, and restore Lua or engine functions (`overrideFunction`, `renameFunction`, `restoreFunction`, `getFunction`, `functionNames`, `LuaApi.addLuaFunction`, `overrideLuaFunction`, `restoreLuaFunction`, `callLuaFunction`, `setLuaVariable`, `getLuaVariable`, `renameLuaFunction`), and lets Lua read/write shared HScript variables (`setHaxeVar`, `getHaxeVar`) and receive `Map`/table return values from `runHaxeCode`. Effort: M.
- **[P2] JSON-driven, mod-extensible Options system (`options/OptionLoader.hx`, `assets/preload/data/options/*.json`).** Built-in options and categories are defined in JSON and hot-reloaded on every menu entry; mods can append custom rows or entire categories via `mods/<mod>/options/<cat>.json` backed by `modSettings[mod]`, with `onChangeLua` / `onChangeHscript` callbacks and `platform` / `define` / `compat` filters. Complements Phoenix's `PlatformSupport` tags and §12.1 ("custom options scripts"). Effort: L.
- **[P2] Psych Engine version compatibility layer (`backend/CompatEngine.hx`) & error-loop silencing (`script/ScriptErrorGuard.hx`).** Selectable `Auto` / `0.6.3` / `0.7.3` / `1.0.4` compatibility profile that adapts version-dependent defaults (`setHealth`, `setObjectCamera`, `loadFrames`, `setAchievementScore`, `deleteFile`, `getDataFromSave` fallback), relaxes Psych 1.0.4 optional parameters, mirrors tagged sounds into `sound_<tag>` in the variables map, and adds `judgementNameCompat` (maps engine-extra top judgement to `'sick'` when read by Psych mods while keeping `Note.ratingRaw` and real engine scoring). `ScriptErrorGuard` silences repeated per-frame error spam from loop callbacks (`onUpdate`, `onBeatHit`, etc.) after `scriptErrorLimit` without killing the script, so `onEndSong`/`onEvent` still fire. Effort: M.
- **[P2] Richer `pack.json` manifest metadata (`backend/ModConfig.hx`).** Phoenix's `backend/Mods.hx` + `ModsMenuState.hx` currently read `name`, `description`, `color`, `restart`, `runsGlobally`, `iconFramerate`, `platforms`, `scriptSandbox`, `scriptSandboxAllowlist`. SeiunEngine adds: `author`, `version`, `apiVersion` (checked against `ENGINE_API_VERSION`, extends §12.6), `downloadLink`, `dependencies` (ordered load, extends §17), `windowTitle` + multi-resolution window icons (`iconPath16/24/32/64`, extends §12.6), `discordClientId` / `discordLogoKey` / `discordLogoText`, `disableWarningScreen`, `disableLanguages`, `useOldFPS`, and `stateReplacements` / `substateReplacements`. Effort: S–M.
- **[P2] Persistent bounded script diagnostics log (`backend/ScriptLog.hx` → `logs/script_log.txt`).** Truncated on boot and capped at 20,000 lines per session; records every scanned script folder (`ok` / `MISSING`), every loaded/failed script, lifecycle callback dispatch (`onCreate`, `onEndSong`, etc.), `getDataFromSave` missing-field fallbacks, and runtime errors with callback name. Complements Phoenix's in-memory `backend/LuaDiagnostics.hx` and `backend/ModHealth.hx`. Effort: S.
- **[P3] Convenience HScript UI wrappers (`script/FunkinSprite.hx`, `FunkinText.hx`, `FunkinBar.hx`, `FunkinButton.hx`).** Lightweight constructors (`loadSparrowAtlas`, `addAnimByPrefix`, `makeSolid`, pre-outlined VCR text, pre-filled `FlxBar` / `FlxButton`) exposed to scripts. Effort: S.
- **[P3] Window close-animation script hooks (desktop).** `onCloseAnimStart(style, speed)`, `onCloseAnimUpdate(progress, style, speed)` (return `{x, y, width, height}` for full control), and `onCloseAnimEnd(style)` in Lua/HScript/Python when the desktop close animation (§31.2) runs. Effort: S.
- **[P3] Online multiplayer script API (`online/backend/OnlineScriptFunctions.hx`).** `sendMessage`, `sendMessageTo`, `listPlayers`, `listPlayersBySide`, `getPlayerSelf`, `getPlayerSelfSID`, `getPlayer`, `getPlayerAccuracy`, `getPlayerRating`, `playsAsBF`, `isRoomOwner`, `hasRoomPerms`, `isRoomConnected`, `getRoomState`, plus `online` / `leftSide` vars. Only relevant if online multiplayer (§31.3) lands.

### 31.2. New Options & Settings

All options below were verified absent from Phoenix's `backend/ClientPrefs.hx` and `source/options/`.

- **Gameplay & Input**
  - **[P2] Sustains as One Note (`guitarHeroSustains`, bool, default off, Gameplay).** Hold notes count as a single hit/miss and cannot be re-caught if dropped (also tracked in §27.3.5). Effort: M.
  - **[P2] Judgement name compatibility (`judgementNameCompat`, bool, default on, Gameplay).** Maps Phoenix's extra `perfect` judgement to `'sick'` when read by Psych-targeted mod scripts (preserving real HUD/scoring and exposing `Note.ratingRaw`), preventing Psych mods that match `rating == 'sick'` from dropping accuracy/combo on Perfect hits. Effort: S.
  - **[P2] Keyboard Feedback / live key overlay (`keyboardDisplay` + `keyboardAlpha`, `keyboardTimeDisplay`, `keyboardTime`, `keyboardBGColor`, `keyboardTextColor`, `KeyboardDisplay.hx`).** Soft-coded on-screen key display with pooled falling key-rain bars, live KPS and total keypress counters, multi-key aware, with exposed script properties + `fullyCustom` mode and `onKeyboardPress`/`onKeyboardRelease`/`onKeyboardUpdate` hooks. Effort: M.
  - **[P3] Last Note Animation (`lastNoteAnimation`, bool, default off, Gameplay).** On simultaneous chord hits, play only the last pressed direction's sing animation to reduce character twitching. Effort: S.
  - **[P3] Save Replay Data (`saveReplayData`, bool, default on, Gameplay).** Paired with replay recording (§14, §31.3).
  - **[P3] Freeplay Auto Music (`freeplayAutoPreview`, bool, default off, General).** Concrete toggle for §22.5's auto-preview entry.
- **Visuals, UI & Localization**
  - **[P2] Localization / Language selector (`language`, `Language.hx`, plus `luattf` `Default TTF` / `Language TTF`).** Full multi-file JSON translation tables (`assets/lang/<Lang>/*.json`) + mod overrides + Psych 1.0.4 `.lang` text parsing + automatic per-language font switching (`Paths.languageFont()`). Phoenix declares `ClientPrefs.locale = 'en'` (`backend/ClientPrefs.hx:63`) but never reads or wires it anywhere. Extends §12.4. Effort: L.
  - **[P2] State Transition style (`transitionStyle`: `Wipe` / `Fade` / `Off`, `backend/transition/TransitionDirector.hx`).** Clean pluggable transition director with instant (`Off`) mode for low-end devices / fast menuing. Extends §0 transitions. Effort: S.
  - **[P2] Note Splash Opacity (`splashAlpha`, percent, Visuals and UI).** Phoenix has `noteSplashes`, `oppNoteSplashes`, `maxSplashLimit`, `splashType`, but no alpha slider for splashes. Effort: S.
  - **[P3] Side HUD (`sidehud`, bool, default on, Visuals and UI).** Toggleable left-side live judgement counters / stats panel that also automatically hides when a mod hides `scoreTxt` (avoiding overlay collisions on scripted menu songs). Effort: S.
  - **[P3] Note Style (`Old` 0.6.3 flat vs `New` 0.7.3+ `noteSkins/`) & Note RGB Shader override (`noteRGBMode`: `Chart` / `On` / `Off`).** Lets players force or disable the RGB note tint shader regardless of chart metadata. Effort: S.
  - **[P3] Pause Menu style (`oldPauseMenu`, bool, Visuals and UI).** Toggle between classic Psych pause menu and redesigned acrylic/blurred 3D-tilt pause menu (§31.3). Effort: M.
  - **[P3] Window Close Animation (`closeAnimStyle`: `off` / `squeeze` / `zoom` / `drop` / `slide`, `closeAnimSpeed`: `0.5x–3.0x`, desktop).** Animated window shrink/slide on Alt+F4 or X button, moddable via `onCloseAnim*` hooks (§31.1). Effort: S.
- **Graphics, Window, Audio & Touch**
  - **[P1] Texture RAM Release (`gfxCpuRelease`, bool, default on, Graphics — `backend/gfx/GfxMemory.hx`).** Releases CPU-side pixel buffers for large textures (≥512×512) after GPU upload and transparently re-decodes via `GfxSource` if read back, saving hundreds of MB of RAM without `cacheOnGPU`'s destructive side effects. Huge fit for Phoenix's low-end identity (§18). Effort: L.
  - **[P2] Separate Update/Draw framerate (`separateUpdateDraw` + `drawFramerate`, Graphics).** Decouples render rate from logic update rate on high-refresh monitors without altering gameplay feel. Concrete implementation for §18 frame pacing. Effort: M.
  - **[P2] Run in Background (`runInBackground`, bool, desktop) & Background Audio Dim (`backgroundDim`, bool, `backend/AudioDim.hx`).** Keep the game running while unfocused (overriding `autoPause`), with a non-destructive focus-loss volume multiplier (`0.25x` in `FlxSound.updateTransform()`) that never mutates or persists `FlxG.sound.volume`. Effort: S.
  - **[P2] Desktop Touch Support (`touchControls`, bool, desktop) + Touch/Swipe Menu Gestures (`touchSwipeEnabled`, bool) + Hitbox refinements (`hitboxPressAlpha`, `hitboxBorder`, `hitboxExtraPos`: `Bottom`/`Top`).** Brings mobile virtual pad / hitbox to PC touchscreens/mouse, adds drag-to-scroll in menus, and makes transparent hitboxes easier to locate. Effort: M.
  - **[P3] Android storage-root selector (`storageType`: `EXTERNAL_DATA` / `EXTERNAL` / `EXTERNAL_OBB` / `EXTERNAL_MEDIA`, `autoExtractAssets`, `showStorageRootWarning`).** Lets Android users choose or inspect storage roots and get a one-time warning when data isn't in user-visible storage (note: compare with Phoenix's SAF `PhoenixDocumentsProvider` before adding legacy `/storage/emulated/0/` paths). Effort: M.
- **FFmpeg Video Renderer extensions (over Phoenix's `options/GameRendererSettingsSubState.hx`)**
  - **[P2] GPU hardware encoders, rate-control modes, preview dry-run & bounded frame buffer (`backend/FFMpeg.hx`, `render.json`).** Phoenix already ships FFmpeg rendering (`ffmpegMode`, `targetFPS`, `unlockFPS`, `renderBitrate`, `vidEncoder`: `libx264`/`libx264rgb`/`libx265`/`libxvid`/`libsvtav1`/`mpeg2video`). SeiunEngine adds: hardware GPU encoders (`h264_nvenc`, `h264_amf`, `h264_qsv`, `hevc_nvenc`) and WebM (`libvpx`/`libvpx-vp9`), `renderMode` (`CRF/CQP` / `VBR` / `CBR`) + `renderQuality` (CRF 0–51), `previewRender` (dry-run timing rehearsal without encoding), `renderAudio` toggle, `renderBufferFrames` (bounded ring buffer capping RAM), `renderOnSongStart` vs F9 mid-song toggle, zero-alloc frame readback, and a dedicated stderr reader thread (preventing Windows pipe deadlocks). Effort: M.
- **Note & Script Optimization Options (see §31.5 & §26)**
  - **[P2] Script optimization toggles (`script_optimization.json`).** `scriptArgReuse` (reuses callback argument arrays via `backend/Scripts.hx` to cut per-hit GC), `stockEventDrain` (O(N) cursor walk vs `Array.shift`), `stockBpmStruct` (reused `Conductor.getBPMFromSeconds()` return object), `ignoreErrorLoopScripts` + `scriptErrorLimit`, `hscriptErrorHandling`. Effort: S–M.
  - **[P2] Extreme-chart / note optimization toggles (`note_optimization.json`, `extra_settings.json`).** `perfMode`, `turboMode` (botplay + pixel-distance ghost-note collapsing), `fastSort` (sort only visible/alive notes), `bulkSkip` (bulk-skip expired notes), `chartCache` + `chartCacheCompress` + `clearChartCache` button + `nativeChartParse`, `segmentedCharts` (`Auto` / `Manifest` / `Off`), `disableGC` (song-local GC pause; Phoenix only has global `enableGC` and render-only `noRenderGC`). Effort: M–L.
  - **[P3] Trace Console options (`traceConsoleEnabled`, `traceConsoleLevel`: `DEBUG`/`INFO`/`WARN`/`ERROR`), `newchartingstate`, `chartAutosave` (off-by-default explicit opt-in), `renderer` (`OpenGL` / experimental `Vulkan`).**

### 31.3. New Game Content, UI & Gameplay Features (engine-generic)

- **[P1] Replay recording, playback & per-song Score History (`Replay.hx`, `Allscore.hx`, `substates/ScoreHistorySubstate.hx`).** Working reference for §14: records per-attempt inputs, resolved judgements, timing windows, `ratingOffset`, `guitarHeroSustains`, `mania`, and hit-offset arrays into `.scores/<song>/<difficulty>/*.json`, browsable from Freeplay with animated score/accuracy counters, hit-offset graph, delete confirmation, and one-click replay playback (plus a "Watch Replay" button on the results screen). Effort: L.
- **[P1] Gamepad rebinding & persistence (`options/ControlsSubState.hx`, `ClientPrefs.gamepadBinds`).** Working reference for §15: adds a keyboard/gamepad toggle column in `ControlsSubState` and persists `gamepadBinds` alongside keyboard binds. Effort: M.
- **[P2] Variable key count 1K–18K (`EKData.hx`).** Extends §25.1 (EK+ only covers 1K–10K): soft-coded via `data/extraKeys/extraKeys.json`, derives extra lane colors dynamically via `ColorSwap` without requiring dedicated 18-key note textures, exposes 1K–18K binds in `ControlsSubState`, supports animated mid-song `changeMania` transitions, and paginates chart-editor grids above 9K. Effort: L.
- **[P2] Built-in GameBanana Mod Browser & Drag-and-Drop Multi-Connection Installer (`online/mods/GameBanana.hx`, `backend/ModDownloader.hx`, `backend/ModInstaller.hx`, `ModZipPlanner.hx`, `ModInstallUI.hx`).** Advances §0 ("built-in mod download support") and upgrades Phoenix's file-picker-only `mobile/files/ModZipInstaller.hx`: listens to desktop `window.onDropFile` (in Phoenix `onDropFile` is only hooked on iOS for deep links in `DeepLinks.hx:35`) to install dropped `.zip` archives, loose mod folders, or `.url`/HTTP links from any state, backed by a 4-connection chunked HTTP range downloader with progress/cancel UI and GameBanana v11 search/category/collection browsing. Effort: L.
- **[P2] Full game-data Backup & Restore (`backend/BackupUtil.hx`, `options/BackupSettingsSubState.hx`).** Extends §13 ("Export/import a save bundle"): bundles highscores, song ratings, `Allscore` history, replays, week completion, prefs and keybinds into a single archive with dedicated Export/Import card UI (Phoenix's `backend/SettingsBackup.hx` only covers prefs, modifiers, binds and audio volume). Prefer standard `.json`/`.zip` over XOR-obfuscated `.SEB` so backups remain inspectable. Effort: M.
- **[P2] Interactive Latency Calibration (`states/LatencyState.hx`).** Visual scrolling notes against a strumline + audio beat test (`LEFT`/`RIGHT` ±1 ms, `SHIFT` ±10 ms, `SPACE` restart) to tune `Conductor.offset`. Advances §15 ("Audio-visual latency calibration"). Effort: S–M.
- **[P2] Online Multiplayer, Embedded LAN Host & Standalone Server (`source/online/**`, `server/**`).** Full Colyseus-compatible multiplayer suite (disabled cleanly via `-D SEIUN_NO_ONLINE`): room browser/join-by-code, real-time & async modes, in-room chat + scrolling `NicommentsView`, live in-game `Scoreboard`, `SkinsState` (online character skin picker with favourites), friends/profile/notifications `SideUI`, online leaderboards, **Funkin Points** (`FunkinPoints.hx` + `ChartAnalyzer.hx` chord-strain/NPS difficulty analyzer, toggleable via `showFP` / `newFPPreview` / F7), **in-client LAN Host** (`online/lan/LanHost.hx` + UDP `LanDiscovery.hx` running the embedded Haxe/SQLite server on a background thread), and a standalone Haxe/Neko/C++ dedicated server (`server/`). Effort: XL (separate multi-PR initiative; keep strictly opt-in / compile-gated).
- **[P2] Live GitHub Contributors & Avatar Cache (`backend/GitHubAPI.hx`, `backend/GitHubCredits.hx`, `tools/gen_contributors.py`).** Concrete implementation for §12.4 ("Lagless GitHub contributors"): instant render from disk cache or bundled `github_contributors.json` snapshot, async live refresh from GitHub API, and TTL-cached avatars. Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] Quick Mod Switcher overlay (`substates/ModSelectSubstate.hx`).** Carousel substate accessible from Main Menu / Freeplay to swap active mod or filter Freeplay songs on the fly (related to §12.3 TAB mod switcher). Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] H-Slice-style Botplay HUD & `Int64` Score/Hit Counters (`states/PlayState.hx`, `Int64Util.hx`).** Replaces the zeroed score line in botplay with `Notes: op + bf = total | NPS: op/max + bf/max = sum/max | HP: %` (auto-compacting to `M`/`B`/`T` past 6 digits so it never wraps), and stores `songScore`, `songHits`, `highestCombo` and judgement totals as `haxe.Int64` so extreme charts never overflow `Int32` (Phoenix currently uses `Float`/`Int`). Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] Acrylic / 3D-Tilt Pause Menu (`substates/PauseSubState.hx`) & Live-Backdrop Results Replay.** Soft-coded translucent glass cards over a blurred live gameplay camera with mouse-driven 3D perspective tilt, toggleable via `oldPauseMenu`. Effort: M.
- **[P3] Unified Animated Boot/Preload State (`states/BootState.hx`, `backend/BootBar.hx`).** Single parametric audio-synced splash + real-work progress bar shared between desktop asset precaching and Android `CopyState` extraction. Effort: S–M.

### 31.4. Editors & Chart Format Converters

- **[P2] Codename Engine (`editors/content/CneExport.hx`) & V-Slice (`editors/content/VSlice.hx`) Chart Import/Export.** Addresses §12.4 ("CNE/modern chart parser"): round-trips CNE 1.6.0 charts (`strumLines`, `noteTypes`, events) and vanilla FNF V-Slice charts + metadata (`timeChanges`, `playData`). Effort: M.
- **[P2] osu!mania & Malody Chart Converter (`editors/ChartConverterState.hx`, `editors/content/OsuMalodyConvert.hx`, `DrMp3.hx`).** Standalone bidirectional converter for `.osu`/`.osz` and `.mc`/`.mcz` ↔ Psych charts (carries over difficulty, background, and music with Auto/4K/8K/Custom key mapping) plus pure-C `dr_mp3` native MP3→16-bit PCM WAV decoding for platforms where Lime/OpenAL cannot decode MP3 directly. Extends §12.4 rhythm-game chart import. Effort: M–L.
- **[P2] Centralized Editor Unsaved-Changes Guard (`backend/UnsavedChangesTracker.hx`).** Appends ` *` to the desktop window title whenever any editor is dirty, intercepts window close (`X`), and shows a unified confirmation prompt before exit, reload, or playtesting. Addresses §12.3 ("unsaved-change warnings"). Effort: S.
- **[P2] Modern Psych 1.0-Style Chart Editor with Classic↔Modern Layout Switch & Custom `PsychUI` Widgets (`editors/NewChartingState.hx`, `editors/chart/ChartEditorLayout.hx`, `backend/ui/PsychUI*.hx`).** Undo/redo stack, animated live toggle between classic 5-tab layout and modern floating panels without duplicating controls, and a self-contained `PsychUI` widget kit replacing `flixel-ui` quirks. Effort: XL.
- **[P3] Two-Pane Credits Editor (`editors/CreditsEditorState.hx`).** Visual editor for `mods/<mod>/data/credits.txt` (sections, spacers, members, icons, links, RGB colors, reorder/add/delete). Effort: M.

### 31.5. Performance, Chart Streaming, Graphics & Engine Infrastructure

- **[P1] Lossless Runtime Texture Optimization Pipeline (`source/backend/gfx/`).** Directly serves Phoenix's low-end / low-RAM focus (§0, §18) without degrading art quality or mutating source files on disk:
  - **`GfxPalette` + `GfxPaletteShader` (L1b):** Any texture whose canvas has ≤256 distinct RGBA values is uploaded as a 1-byte index texture + 256×1 RGBA palette and expanded with `NEAREST` sampling in the fragment shader — **4× VRAM savings with bit-exact rendering**.
  - **`GfxAtlasCrop` (L2a):** Crops spritesheet bitmaps to the union bounding box of all frame rects, updates UVs, samples pixels to verify identity, and rolls back on any mismatch.
  - **`GfxAtlasRepack` (L2b):** Trims transparent padding, deduplicates identical frames by hash, and repacks deterministically using a persistent `GfxRepackPlan`.
  - **`GfxMemory` (`gfxCpuRelease`):** Drops CPU pixel buffers after GPU upload for large textures (≥512×512) and re-decodes lazily via `GfxSource` if anything accesses pixels later.
  - **`GfxCaps`:** One-shot GL compressed-texture capability probe (`DXT1/3/5`, `ETC1/2`, `BC6H/BC7`, `ASTC`) for diagnostics/future compressed texture work.
  - Effort: L (land incrementally: `GfxMemory` → `GfxAtlasCrop` → `GfxPalette` → `GfxAtlasRepack`).
- **[P1] Extreme-Scale Chart Pipeline (`ChartNotes`, `ChartStream`, `ChartPrefetch`, `ChartCache`, `ChartNative`, `ChartPack`, `ChartParts`, `TurboDensity`).** Concrete, production-tested Haxe architecture for §26 (with actual Haxe integration, unlike the JS-Engine C++-only branch in §26):
  - **Columnar SoA `ChartNotes.hx` with scalar hoisting + Lua row pinning (`liveAt(i)`):** Columns where every note shares one value never allocate a backing array; Lua `setPropertyFromGroup('unspawnNotes', i, ...)` pins only the mutated row so custom notetype scripts keep full Psych compatibility!
  - **Byte-streaming scanner (`ChartStream.hx`) + bounded async section prefetcher (`ChartPrefetch.hx`):** Scans giant (64 MB+) JSONs into a skeleton DOM + section byte ranges, then parses chunks on a worker thread (capped resident memory) while the main thread builds notes.
  - **Compressed disk cache (`ChartCache.hx`) + optional native C++ scanner/folder (`ChartNative.hx`):** Constant-column packed + deflated cache (measured 290 MB → 2 MB) with `Clear Chart Cache` button.
  - **Segmented / Multi-File Charts (`ChartParts.hx`):** Plays split `<song>-0..N.json` or explicit `<song>.parts.json` manifests as one seamless song, with `Options -> Advanced -> Multi-file Charts` (`Auto` / `Manifest` / `Off`) and per-song Freeplay override (`M`).
  - **Deduplicated `.chartpack` container (`ChartPack.hx` + `tools/chartpack/chartpack.mjs`) & Turbo ghost-note collapsing (`TurboDensity.hx`):** Collapses same-lane overlapping taps within a screen-pixel threshold into density-weighted representatives for million-note spam charts.
  - Effort: L (pure-Haxe `ChartNotes` + `ChartStream` + `ChartCache` first; keep `#if sys` fallbacks for Web).
- **[P1] Async LibVLC / hxvlc Warmup (`backend/VideoPreloader.hx`, `backend/VideoSpriteManager.hx`).** Runs `Handle.initAsync()` + dummy media pipeline prewarm during boot/loading so the first video cutscene doesn't freeze the main thread. Phoenix's `objects/VideoSprite.hx` currently initializes synchronously on first playback. Effort: S–M.
- **[P1] Native C++ Crash Hooks + Unified Symbolization Tool (`backend/NativeCrash.hx`, `native_crash.inc`, `states/CrashCatcherState.hx`, `tools/crash.py`, `tools/SymbolsAfterBuild.hx`).** Phoenix's `backend/CrashHandler.hx` only catches Haxe `UncaughtErrorEvent`s — native segfaults, GPU driver faults, and LuaJIT aborts die silently. SeiunEngine installs Windows SEH and POSIX (`SIGSEGV`/`SIGABRT`/`SIGBUS`/`SIGFPE`/`SIGILL`) handlers that capture registers, module+RVA backtraces, `/proc/self/maps`, precomputed binary address→`file:line` linemaps (Android `.so` + MSVC `.pdb`), and a unified `tools/crash.py` triage/symbolization CLI+GUI with build-stamp verification (complements Phoenix's `CrashBundle`). Effort: L.
- **[P2] Runtime Stability & Rendering Hardening.**
  - **`backend/GroupGuard.hx`:** Lightweight hxcpp vtable integrity sweep before `FlxTypedGroup.update()` that nulls dangling/freed member slots and logs the offender instead of crashing with an `ACCESS_VIOLATION` inside Flixel's update loop. Effort: S–M.
  - **`backend/GlErrorWatchdog.hx`:** Throttled `glGetError()` polling on `Event.RENDER` logged into the trace ring buffer so GPU/shader faults show up in crash dumps. Effort: S.
  - **`backend/FontRegistry.hx`:** Fallback bundled-font lookup + `openfl.text.Font.registerFont()` when the working directory and asset manifest disagree (prevents silent fallback to system fonts). Effort: S.
  - **`backend/RenderScale.hx`:** Device-pixel/DPI-aware `BaseScaleMode` (reads Context3D backbuffer vs stage display matrix) fixing Retina/3× HiDPI clipping offsets and supporting cover (`fillScreen`) mode. Compare against Phoenix's `PhoenixScaleMode` before porting. Effort: M.
  - **`mohong/TraceConsole.hx` + `TraceManager.hx`:** Live ANSI-colored trace console (Win32 console window / POSIX stdout / Android logcat) with severity filter and ring buffer fed into crash reports. Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] SDL3 backend (`lime-sdl3`) & Experimental Vulkan Renderer (`-D seiun_vulkan`, `backend/VulkanNotice.hx`, `options/RendererShowcase.hx`).** Worth tracking long-term for desktop/Android input, audio hotplug and GPU performance, but requires upstream Lime/OpenFL support rather than adopting SeiunEngine's custom haxelib forks (see §31.7).

### 31.6. Checked and Already Covered / Superseded in Phoenix (do not port)

- **Visual Stage Editor:** Already ships in Phoenix (`editors/StageEditorState.hx`, `docs/STAGE_EDITOR.md`, landed in #355/#358).
- **Results screen with hit-offset histogram:** Already ships in Phoenix (`states/ResultsState.hx` + `ResultsData.hx`); only the blurred live backdrop and replay-playback hook (§31.3) are missing.
- **Window mode (`Windowed` / `Borderless` / `Fullscreen`):** Done (`backend/WindowMode.hx`, Options → Graphics).
- **Menu & focus-lost framerate caps:** Done (`backend/FramerateController.hx`, `menuFramerate`, `focusLostFramerate`).
- **Lane underlay & Stage dim:** Done (`play/helpers/PlayStateLaneUnderlay.hx`, `PlayStateStageDim.hx`).
- **Accuracy / judgement timing presets:** Done (`backend/AccuracyPresets.hx`).
- **Metronome & Gitaroo pause easter egg:** Done (`backend/Metronome.hx`, `ClientPrefs.gitarooEasterEgg`).
- **Linux Feral GameMode:** Done (`Main.hx` `hxgamemode.GamemodeClient` + `gamemode.ini`).
- **Windows dark titlebar:** Done (`backend/WindowColorMode.hx`).
- **Crash `.zip` bundle & safe-mode recovery screen:** Done (`backend/CrashBundle.hx`, `states/RecoveryState.hx`, `backend/RecoveryBoot.hx`); only native C++ SEH/signal hooks + symbolization (§31.5) are missing.
- **Settings `.json` backup/restore:** Done (`backend/SettingsBackup.hx`); only full save-bundle export including scores/replays (§31.3, §13) is missing.
- **`.zip` mod importer:** Done (`mobile/files/ModZipInstaller.hx` via file picker); only desktop drag-and-drop and HTTP/GameBanana downloads (§31.3) are missing.
- **FFmpeg base video rendering:** Done (`options/GameRendererSettingsSubState.hx`, `backend/FFmpegLocator.hx`); only the GPU encoders, CRF/VBR/CBR rate control, preview dry-run, and bounded ring buffer (§31.2) are missing.
- **Rendered-note cap & FPS/RAM/Benchmark diagnostics:** Done (`ClientPrefs.maxNotes`, `debug/FPSCounter.hx`, `debug/profiler/FrameProfiler.hx`, `backend/MemoryLedger.hx`, `backend/NativeHardware.hx`, `backend/Benchmark.hx`, `backend/ModHealth.hx`).
- **HScript `ModState`/`ModSubState` host:** Done (`states/ModState.hx`, `states/substates/ModSubState.hx`, `psychlua/StateScripts.hx`); only Lua support and `pack.json` `stateReplacements`/`substateReplacements` (§31.1) are missing.
- **Phoenix-unique strengths SeiunEngine lacks:** Native 34-modifier modchart engine + Modchart Lab (`source/modchart/`), Python softmodding (`PythonScript`), script security scanner & sandbox (`ModSecurity`, `ScriptSecurityScanner`), full Accessibility suite (`AccessibilitySubState`, colorblind note palettes, luminance contrast, lane glyphs, hold-to-confirm, visual beat indicator), extra unlimited vocal stems per strumline (`docs/EXTRA_VOCALS.md`), Pause Photo Mode, Device Benchmark mode, `phoenix://` deep-link URIs, OpenFL screen filters + chainable sprite effects API, Android Media3/SAF/cutout/haptics integration, and HTML5/Web target work.

### 31.7. Excluded Engine/Mod-Specific Content & Non-Goals (do not port)

- **Excluded mod/engine-specific content:** Any SeiunEngine or *Mandela Funkin Night* weeks, songs, characters, stages, credits entries, branding/logos, `TestBuildNoticeState`, and Seiun watermarks.
- **License-incompatible shader (`shaders/WarpShader.hx`):** Carries `Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial-ShareAlike 3.0 Unported` — **incompatible with Phoenix's Apache 2.0 license**; do not port.
- **Do not weaken Phoenix's `ModSecurity` sandbox.** SeiunEngine exposes `sys.io.File`, `sys.FileSystem`, and `sys.io.Process` directly to HScript (`script/hscript/HScript.hx:598-602`). Any ported HScript/reflection APIs (`createInstance`, `callMethod`, `LuaApi`, `require`/`import`) must go through `ModSecurity` and `ScriptSecurityScanner`.
- **Do not adopt SeiunEngine's 9 custom haxelib forks (`hmm.json`).** SeiunEngine maintains private forks of `flixel`, `flixel-addons`, `flixel-ui`, `hxvlc`, `extension-androidtools`, `linc_luajit-rewriten`, `openfl`, `lime`, and `hscript`. Port the engine-side features in pure Haxe or against upstream haxelibs so Phoenix keeps maintainable dependencies and Web/HashLink compatibility (`#if sys` guards everywhere).
- **Dead/stub files skipped:** `backend/RenderThread.hx` (disabled single-thread stub) and `ButtonRemapSubstate.hx` (empty stub).

## 32. SUPER PLUS ENGINE PARITY REVIEW — `atox07/FNF-Super-Plus-Engine`

**Source:** [atox07/FNF-Super-Plus-Engine](https://github.com/atox07/FNF-Super-Plus-Engine/tree/main) at `8679026` (2026-11-09). It is **Plus Engine** ([LeninAsto/FNF-PlusEngine](https://github.com/LeninAsto/FNF-PlusEngine), itself Psych **1.0.4** based) plus AtoX's own additions — so §5.3 of [`docs/research/psych-fork-engine-changes.md`](docs/research/psych-fork-engine-changes.md) is the upstream half of this review, and everything here is the delta **this** fork adds on top, checked against our tree at `443ae01`.

**License:** Apache 2.0, same as ours → **every entry below is portable** under the rule at the top of this file. Credit `AtoX` (Super Plus), `LeninAsto`/`sirthegamercoder` (Plus Engine), `Autistic Lulu` (Psych Continued) and `Mrlordnoob/Slushi` per feature, the way SPE's own `CreditsState` does. Their README is explicit that code is meant to be learned from and reused ("Learning > hoarding"); do not port the *branding*, only the mechanisms.

**Method:** cloned the repo and read it source-to-source against this tree; nothing was built or run on either side. _**Numbering note:** this review was written as §27, and has been renumbered twice as other reviews landed on `main` first (§27 Mixtape, then §28–§31 FunkinAVI / Yarn / Doors / Seiun). All internal cross-references (`32.x`) were updated each time. If it is ever moved again, the whole `32.` prefix in this section is what to change._

**Re-verified against `main` after the merge that brought in §27 (Mixtape) and the Stage-Editor work** — three entries changed as a result and say so inline (Stage Editor is no longer missing; the stage `preload` map is parsed but never consumed; Photo mode is done). Lua API names were diffed mechanically (`Lua_helper.add_callback` / `Convert.addCallback` vs our `registerFunction` / `addCallback` **and** the `Py*Lib`/HScript mirror sets): SPE registers **484** names, we register **420**, and **253** SPE names have no equivalent here — but a large share of those are SPE-side wrappers around things Phoenix already exposes under a different name, or are *declared and never read* in SPE itself. Both cases are called out per entry; do not port dead toggles.

**Honest headline:** SPE is **bigger on editors, on one long list of HUD/gameplay toggles, on i18n and on Windows-native scripting**; Phoenix is far ahead on shaders, filters, chart-format breadth, accessibility, security, low-end performance and test/spec discipline. Below is only what we actually lack.

---

### 32.1. SCRIPTING & MODDING API (the 253-name delta, grouped)

Names below were verified absent from `source/` by exact-string search. Everything registered goes through `psychlua/callbacks/*` and is inherited by Lua, Python and HScript at once (`PythonScript.hx:388`, `HScript.hx:249`), so **one registration covers all three languages** — that is why these are cheap.

- **[P1] Script-local variable store — `setVar`/`getVar`/`setGlobalVar`/`getGlobalVar` (+`getValue`/`setValue`).** SPE registers all five in `psychlua/backend/FunkinLua.hx` and they back its whole "share state between scripts" story (`getGlobalFromScript` only crosses *named* scripts here, `callbacks/ScriptCallbacks.hx:107`). Ours has no per-script key/value bag at all. Add one `Map<String,Dynamic>` per `FunkinLua` instance with a `global` flag for the shared one, expose in all three languages, and clear it on script close. Fits §12.1's "managed runtime scripts" entry. Effort: S.
- **[P1] State/substate scripting surface — `switchToState`, `exitToState`, `getStateName`, `getParentStateName`, `callStateFunction`, `callSubstateFunction`, `openScriptedSubstate`, `getScriptHostType`, `getLuaMode`, `isRawLua`, `launchMod`, `exitToEngine`, `setExitTarget`.** We have `switchCustomState`/`openModSubState`/`customStateExists` (`psychlua/CustomSubstate.hx:21-23`) and nothing else: a script cannot ask *which* state it is in, cannot call a function by name on that state, and cannot relaunch a mod or return to the engine from a mod. SPE's `MenuScripts` + `ScriptableState` pair makes menu scripting a first-class scope (see §32.2). Effort: M for the introspection half (`getStateName`/`getParentStateName`/`call*Function`), M for `launchMod`/`exitToEngine`.
- **[P1] `callMethod` / `callMethodFromClass` / `createInstance` / `addInstance` / `instanceArg`.** SPE's `ReflectionFunctions.hx` (368 lines) lets a script invoke any public method on any object or class, and instantiate a class with arbitrary ctor args. We deliberately ship `Reflect.callMethod`/`Type.createInstance` as **blocked patterns in the security scanner** (`backend/ScriptSecurityScanner.hx:98`) and have no replacement, so a mod that needs `Character.new(...)` or `FlxTween.num(...)` cannot run here at all. Port it *as a sandboxed allowlist* — a per-class set of `{method, arity, arg types}` in `backend.ModSecurity`, defaulting to the Flx/engine classes mods actually use, with the scanner still blocking the raw `Reflect`/`Type` forms. That keeps the §6 security posture while closing a real capability gap. Effort: M.
- **[P2] Camera scroll API — `setCameraScroll`, `addCameraScroll`, `getCameraScrollX`, `getCameraScrollY`, `setCameraFollowPoint`, `addCameraFollowPoint`, `getCameraFollowX`, `getCameraFollowY`, `getCameraScroll*` on the modchart side.** `callbacks/CameraCallbacks.hx` only has `createCamera`/`addCamera`/`removeCamera`, and `callbacks/GameCallbacks.hx:223-243` has `cameraSetTarget`/`cameraShake`/`cameraFlash`/`cameraFade` — **camera position is not scriptable at all**. Every scroll/parallax mod chart needs this. Effort: S.
- **[P2] Object screen-space math — `getObjectScreenX`/`Y`/`Width`/`Height`, `gameToScreenX`/`Y`, `getScreenWidth`/`Height`/`Resolution`.** We have `getScreenPositionX/Y` and `getMidpointX/Y` (`callbacks/SpriteCallbacks.hx`) but not the object's *rendered* box or the game→screen transform, which is what touch hit-testing and multi-window drawing need. Effort: S.
- **[P2] `removeFromGroup` companion — `addToGroup`.** `PropertyCallbacks.hx:104` registers `removeFromGroup` but there is no way to *add* to a group from a script. One-sided. Effort: S.
- **[P2] Colour helpers — `getColorFromString`, `getColorFromName`.** `backend.CoolUtil.colorFromString` exists (`CoolUtil.hx:439`) but is not registered for scripts; mods must hardcode ARGB ints. Effort: S.
- **[P2] `updateScoreText`.** Force a score-text refresh after a script changes score/misses — today scripts must wait for the next hit. Effort: S.
- **[P2] Sound pitch — `getSoundPitch` / `setSoundPitch`.** `callbacks/SoundCallbacks.hx` covers volume/time/fade but not pitch, so a script cannot do the slowed/sped-up voice gimmick that SPE mods rely on (`playbackRate` on a `FlxSound` is the hook). Effort: S.
- **[P3] Text extras — `setTextAutoSize`, `setTextHeight`.** `callbacks/TextCallbacks.hx` has `setTextWidth` but not height/auto-size. Trivial. Effort: S.
- **[P3] FlxAnimate extras — `setFlxAnimateAutoDeactivate` / `deactivateFlxAnimateSprite`.** We ship `makeFlxAnimateSprite`, `loadAnimateAtlas`, `addAnimationBySymbol(Indices)` (`psychlua/FlxAnimateFunctions.hx:39,54`) — i.e. **more** SpriteMat symbol support than SPE — but no way to park an idle atlas off the GPU. Matters on phones with several atlases. Effort: S.

#### 32.1.1. Windows-native scripting (desktop-only, `sys && windows`)

- **[P2] Window geometry & lifecycle — `setWindowSize`/`X`/`Y`/`Pos`, `getWindowWidth`/`Height`/`X`/`Y`, `getGameWindowWidth`/`Height`/`X`/`Y`, `centerWindow`, `setWinRCenter`, `randomizeWindowPosition`, `setWindowResizable`, `setWindowVisible`, `setWindowAlwaysOnTop`, `setWindowBackground`, `setWindowBorderColor`, `setWindowIcon`, `setWindowBorderless`, `hideWindowBorder`, `setWindowView`, `windowExists`, `getWindowState`, `saveWindowState`/`loadWindowState`, `getWindowTitle`/`setWindowTitle`, `getWindowOpacity`/`setWindowOpacity`, `tweenWindowOpacity`, `winTweenSize`/`winTweenX`/`winTweenY`, `tweenWindowBorderColor`, `minimizeWindow`.** **`WindowCallbacks.hx` is 124 lines and registers exactly nine names**: the four transparency/click-through calls plus `applyWindowTransparency`, and one `setWindowFullscreen`. Everything else — size, position, opacity, border, icon, always-on-top, and the whole `winTween*` easing set (`psychlua/backend/WindowTweens.hx`, 884 lines upstream) — is missing even though `backend/Windows.hx`/`WindowNative.hx` already do the native work for transparency. This is the single largest *cheap* API gap in the review: port `WindowTweens` almost verbatim, gate each call on `PlatformSupport.DESKTOP` + `#if windows`, and default every effect off per the porting rule. Effort: M (mostly transcription).
- **[P2] Multi-window ("outside the window" / Unresponsive-style) — `createWindow`, `closeWindow`, `closeAllWindows`, `addSpriteToWindow`, `removeSpriteFromWindow`, `setWindowView`, `setWindowAlwaysOnTop`.** SPE's `psychlua/MultiWindowFunctions.hx` (478 lines) plus `WinNative.hx` (310 lines of Win32) open extra layered topmost windows that never steal focus and redraw chosen game sprites into them each frame. We have the *transparent-window* half (`backend/FlxTransWindow.hx`, click-through) but only on the main HWND. This is what the Unresponsive-style gag mods are built on, and it is genuinely absent here. Effort: **L** (Win32 C++ + per-frame BitmapData readback; do it after the geometry API above, which it reuses).
- **[P3] Screen/desktop info — `getScreenWidth`/`Height`/`Resolution`, `getGameWindow*`, `captureScreenshot`, `getDesktopWindowsXPos`/`Y`, `getWindowsVersion`.** `captureScreenshot` is a real gap: our `backend/SSPlugin` only fires from its F2 key check (`SSPlugin.hx:34,129`), so a script cannot take a shot. Wire one public entry point (see §22.5's auto-screenshot entry, which wants the same call). Effort: S.
- **[P3] Clipboard & notifications for scripts — `clipboardGetText`/`clipboardSetText`/`clipboardHasText`, `showNotification`.** We have `PlatformUtil.sendNotification` + a `Windows Notification` chart event and `docs/DESKTOP_NOTIFICATIONS.md`, but **no script binding** and no clipboard binding; SPE has both, and its clipboard helpers are what its "copy this Lua snippet" editors use. Effort: S.
- **[P3] `showToast` / `setActivityTitle` / `setOrientation` / `isScreenKeyboardShown` / `manualBackButton` on mobile.** Our `android/platform/AndroidStdFire.hx:65` already implements `showToast`, and `AndroidNotification`/`AndroidDisplay`/`AndroidIntents` have the rest of the native calls — **none of it is reachable from Lua/Python/HScript.** SPE's `mobile/psychlua/Functions.hx` (48 registrations) is exactly that binding layer. Add a `psychlua/callbacks/MobileCallbacks.hx` that calls the modules we already own. Effort: M, high payoff on Android.
- **[P3] Android device probes — `isRooted`, `isTablet`, `isChromebook`, `isDeXMode`, `isDolbyAtmos`, `isAndroidTV`.** Ours has `AndroidSystem.isAndroidTV`/`getDeviceInfo` and `AndroidTVPolicy`; the other five are unimplemented even natively. Mostly `Build.*` reads through the existing JNI bridge; low value, listed for completeness. Effort: S.
- **[P3] `getSoundPitch`/`setSoundPitch`, `getChartNotes`, `getBPM`/`getCurrentBeat`/`getCurrentStep`.** `getBPM`/beat/step are not script functions here (only `curStep`/`curBeat` *variables* pushed at `PlayState.hx:3372,3417`), and `getChartNotes` — read the whole chart from a script — has no equivalent. `getChartNotes` is the useful one: it turns a modchart script into a real chart-driven tool. Effort: S.

#### 32.1.2. The ActorFrame sprite-tree API

- **[P3] `_actorFrame*` (11 names) — a nested "actor frame" object model.** `_actorFrameCreate/Destroy/Set/Get/SetColor/Tween/CancelTweens/SetShader/SetTarget/Capture/Exists` in `psychlua/ActorFrameFunctions.hx` + a 4-file backend including a **Lua prelude** (`ActorFrameLuaPrelude.hx`) that builds a small OOP wrapper in Lua itself, plus `ActorFrameTextureSource` for render-to-texture capture. It is a step-game-style container hierarchy (parent tags, per-frame capture, group tween). Phoenix's `ModchartSprite` + groups cover the flat case; the *hierarchy with captured textures* does not exist. Port only if a real mod asks — the whole thing is ~1.2k lines for a use case our `createCamera` + `sprite` pipeline mostly covers. Effort: L. **Priority deliberately low.**

---

### 32.2. EDITORS — the biggest real gap

SPE's `MasterEditorMenu` lists **ten** editors (`states/editors/MasterEditorMenu.hx:13-24`: chart, character, stage, week, **hud**, menu character, dialogue, dialogue **portrait**, dialogue **pixel**, note splash); two more are reachable from inside the chart editor (**Event Creator**, `ChartingState.hx:4971`; **Cover Maker**, `:4960`) and a third from the master menu (**Modchart Converter**, `MasterEditorMenu.hx:169`). Ours has five (`editors/MasterEditorMenu.hx:19-25`: Menu Character, Character, Chart, Modchart Lab, Note Splash *Debug*). Below is everything they have that we do not.

- **[P1] HUD / layout editor (`states/editors/HudEditorState.hx`, 4,173 lines).** Drag the native HUD widgets (healthBar, scoreTxt, timeBar/timeTxt, comboGroup, both strum lines), import PNG overlays, reorder by zIndex, group-move (Strums Opponent / Boyfriend / All), and **record chained keyframe tweens** per element (A→B→C…) with per-segment duration/easing, then **export the whole thing as a Lua layout script** — overlays into `onCreate()`, moved strums into `onCountdownStarted()`, chained tweens into `onBeatHit`/`onStepHit`, draw order as `setObjectOrder` calls. Everything lives in `camHUD` only. We have nothing: `play/PlayState.hx` hardcodes HUD layout and the only way to move a widget is a script written by hand. This is the single highest-value port in the whole review — it turns hours of trial-and-error scripting into drag-and-drop. Their header documents the traps that will bite us too: strums are created in `startCountdown()`, *after* `onCreatePost` (so static strum positions must go in `onCountdownStarted()`); icons follow the health bar every frame and cannot be moved; `healthBar`/`timeBar`/`comboGroup` are groups with no reliable scale/angle; `missesTxt`/`accuracyTxt`/`timeBarBG`/`albumArt` do not exist as separate widgets in their build. Port the *generator* pattern first — the accessibility work in §16 wants a layout layer, and Photo mode (§21.1, now DONE) shows the HUD can already be hidden wholesale. 4K-only is fine initially — it matches our `play/helpers/PlayStateNoteHelpers.hx:257` (`for (i in 0...4)`). Effort: **L**, and worth it.
- **[P2] Cover Maker — an in-game mini-DAW (`states/editors/CoverMaker.hx` 2,308 lines + `CoverMidi`/`CoverVoice`; opened as a *substate* of the chart editor, `ChartingState.hx:4960`, which is why it is easy to miss in their menu).** Piano-roll timeline for Dad/BF/Instrumental lanes, MIDI import for the vocal parts, per-note preview and slice count, exports a `Voices`/`Voices-Player` pair so a mod can ship a mashup cover **without leaving the game**. We have no audio-authoring tool at all. Effort: **L**. Cheap half worth doing first: the MIDI **reader** (`CoverMidi`), which also feeds the chart editor's import below.
- **[P2] Event Creator (`states/editors/EventCreatorState.hx` 2,341 lines + a 4-file `eventcreator/` backend; opened from the chart editor, `ChartingState.hx:4971`).** A **visual editor for custom Lua events**: name/description/category, typed parameters (`text,int,float,bool,slider,dropdown,character,color,sound,image,stage` — the last five enumerated from actual game folders via `EventPresets`), a block-based body with a palette, `EventCodegen` emitting the `.lua`, and a **`Regenerate`** button that rewrites the chart's event list when a parameter is renamed. This solves a real hole: today a mod author hand-writes both the script and the event JSON, and they drift. Our `editors/charting/ChartingEvents.hx` only *consumes* event names. Effort: M–L. Pairs with §17's mod tooling and §21.1's Extras hub.
- **[P2] MIDI → chart import (`ChartingState.addMidiPicker` / `scanMidiFolder` / `parseMidi`, ~700 lines inside their 8,502-line chart editor).** Pick a `.mid` from `assets/midi/` or **Browse…**, then **Import as BF / as Dad / as Dad+BF / Auto split**, converting pitch→lane and length→sustain with a documented `MIDI_SUSTAIN_MIN_STEPS` threshold, plus a warning list for BPM changes the chart can't represent. We have **zero** MIDI code and no `moonchart` dependency (`hmm.json` has none; §12.4 already asked for `.sm`/`.ssc` import as a separate entry — this is the `.mid` sibling, and the same importer would serve SPE's OGG-pitch-detection path too). Effort: M.
- **[P2] Character editor — "Anims from XML" (`CharacterEditorState.hx:697`, XML picker at `:1698-1710`).** Pick any `.xml`+`.png` pair from `images/characters/`, get every frame name, and **auto-generate the character's `anims` array** (and its `flip_x`/offsets guesses) in place. Our character editor requires typing each animation by hand. This is a small, self-contained, high-annoyance-saving feature. Effort: S–M.
- **[P2] ~~Stage editor with drag-and-drop.~~ NO LONGER A GAP** — `main` merged `editors/StageEditorState.hx` (1,674 lines) + `data/StageEditorCore.hx` (886) with real dragging (`StageEditorState.hx:1528-1547`), `Ctrl` for free-drag, grid snapping, and `_editorMeta` round-tripping (`:763-765`). Theirs is still bigger (4,550 lines) — the concrete deltas worth a follow-up are **multi-select boxes**, **right-click deselect / click-again-to-cycle overlapping sprites**, and per-object **beat-sync + scroll-factor editing**. Effort: S–M as a patch to what we now have.
- **[P2] Dialogue pixel editor (`DialoguePixelEditorState.hx`, 670 lines).** Week 6 pixel dialogue, with **the real `DialogueBoxPixel` as the live preview** (WYSIWYG), portrait lookup from `images/custom_dialogs/dialogPortraits/`, add/delete/reorder lines, per-line expression, saves to `data/<song>/dialogue-pixel.json`. Our Master Editor has a plain Dialogue Editor but no pixel variant, and W6 is shipped content. Effort: M.
- **[P3] Note Splash editor *parity*.** We ship `NoteSplashDebugState` (offset nudging, min/max FPS, double-tap ENTER save). SPE's `NoteSplashEditorState` (1,093 lines) additionally does: **`Convert TXT`** (turn a legacy `noteSplashes.txt` into the modern config), **Allow RGB / Allow Pixel** toggles, **`Add/Update` a named animation** with its own prefix, and a **`Color to Replace`** shader pass. The three that matter are `Convert TXT` (migration for old mods) and the two allowance flags. Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] Chart editor — five missing tabs' worth of tooling.** Ours has 7 tabs (`ChartingState.hx:515-521`); theirs has 10 (`addChartingTab`, `addDataTab`, `addEventsTab`, `addNoteTab`, `addSectionTab`, `addSongTab`, **`addMetaTab`**, **`addFileTab`**, **`addEditTab`**, **`addViewTab`**). Concretely missing here:
  - **Edit tab** (the highest-value one): `Ctrl+C/X/V/A` on a note selection box, `Ctrl+Z`/`Ctrl+Y` undo **stacks** (`addUndoAction(ADD_NOTE/…)` with grouped actions), Cut/Copy/Paste of notes **and events**. We have `undos`/`redos` arrays and `ChartingSaveLoad.undo()` but **no copy/paste/cut at all** and no grouped undo. This is the most-used editing shortcut set in any charter. Effort: M.
  - **View tab**: waveform target switch (inst / player vox / opp vox), zoom in/out, **section jump list** (`Jump Section`), toggle grid/sections/events column.
  - **Meta tab**: a song-meta form (see §32.10) with license/link rows.
  - **File tab**: **Import MIDI…**, **New Chart**, **Browse…** file pickers for chart/events JSON, drop-a-JSON-onto-the-window loading (`'Drop JSON file here to load chart or events'`).
  - **Data tab**: `PreloadListSubState` — a per-chart **preload manifest editor** (see §32.10).
  Effort: M for Edit+View (highest value), S each for the rest.
- **[P3] Modchart Converter (`ModchartConverterState.hx`, 172 lines).** Paste an NVME/older-style Lua modchart on the clipboard, get a converted first pass with a preview pane, plus warnings about what it cannot translate ("reassign the steps, remove the NVMe radian helpers, `fieldYaw` is the Plus-side name"). Small, but it is the on-ramp for mods written against other modchart stacks. Effort: S.
- **[P3] Scriptable menu states — "Menu Scripting".** With `HSCRIPT_ALLOWED`, `MainMenuState` loads `scripts/menus/MainMenuState/init.lua|.hx`, and `FreeplayStateSelector` picks `FreeplayState_Psych` or a script-supplied `FreeplayState` per launched mod (`backend/ScriptableState`, `scripting/ScriptedStates`). We have `psychlua/StateScripts` (overlay-only, `data/states/<State>.hx`) and `ModState`/`ModSubState` as blank hosts — **no way to replace a whole menu**, and no Lua menu scripting at all. §12.1 lists "scripted states" only as *script scopes*; this is the concrete mechanism worth copying, including their `FreeplayStateSelector` indirection pattern. Effort: M.

---

### 32.3. OPTIONS & PREFERENCES

Thirty-odd keys exist in SPE's `ClientPrefs` with **no counterpart here** (`grep` of our `ClientPrefs.hx` for each: all missing). Sorted by whether SPE actually *reads* them — several do not, and those are marked.

**Read by SPE and genuinely absent here:**

- **[P1] `shadedTimeBar` — "Gradient Time Bar".** Tints the bar's fill with the **opponent's icon colour** and the empty part with **BF's** (`PlayState.hx:1051`: `timeBar.setColors(dadColor, bfColor)`), so it reads as a tug-of-war. `timeBarType` itself already exists here (`ClientPrefs.hx:111`, values `Time Left` / …), so this is *only* the tinting (`shadedTimeBar`, `VisualsSettingsSubState.hx:109`). One pref, two `setColors` calls at build time and again on icon swap. Effort: S.
- **[P2] `breakTimer` — "Break Timer" HUD.** Shows a countdown/timer when the next notes are far away (their `states/play/BreakTimerHud.hx`, drawn with an MD3 wavy progress ring). `breakTimer` is turned on at `PlayState.hx:2352-2353` through `breakTimerHud.updateDisplay(Conductor.songPosition, startingSong, playerStrums, downScroll, strumCenterX, strumTopY)`. Nothing like it here; useful on spam-mod charts where the next note is 8 seconds away.
- **[P2] `showKeyViewer` + `keyViewerColor` + `keyViewerOffset`, and the `objects/KeyViewer.hx` behind them (691 lines).** A **pressure-bar key viewer**: per-lane key rectangle, KPS counter, total-press counter, growing pressure bars, centring, colour presets with contrast-aware text colour, music-beat animation, and it re-labels itself live when the binds change. We have **no key viewer object whatsoever**, and `showKeyViewer`/`keyViewerColor`/`keyViewerOffset` do not exist as fields in our `ClientPrefs.hx` (verified by `find source -ipath '*keyviewer*'` → empty, `grep -r keyViewer source/` → 0). Their `NoteOffsetState` also embeds the viewer as a live preview of the offset you are editing, which is the better half of the idea. Effort: M; the pressure bars can start as plain rectangles.
- **[P2] `dynamicCombo` (combo digits grow with the number instead of always 3 wide), `nfRatingStyle` (ratings/combo "bop in place" instead of fly-and-fade), `timeBump` (kill the time-text bump), `showVersionText` (song + difficulty + build on the gameplay HUD), `endCountdown`/`endCountSecs` (last-seconds countdown), `pauseCountdown` (3-2-1 on unpause), `heyIntro` (BF/GF auto-"Hey!" on GO — never read, see the note at the end of this subsection).** All six that are left are read at real call sites in SPE: `dynamicCombo` (`PlayState.hx:3759` — `combo.lpad('0',3)` vs the raw number), `nfRatingStyle` (`:3648`, `:3712` — bop in place instead of fly-and-fade), `timeBump` (`:1832`), `showVersionText` (`:676`), `endCountdown` (`:2372`, `:3491`), `pauseCountdown` (`:2193`). We have `comboStacking`, `simplePopups`, `iconBounceType`, `ratingType`, `showNPS` — different axes. **Two of the six are already spoken for here**: `pauseCountdown` is our `resumeCountdown` (§21.3, DONE — *check whether it already matches their 3-2-1 behaviour before adding anything*), and the intro `countdownStyle` entry in §22.4 covers the same ground as their `endCountdown` sibling. That leaves **`dynamicCombo`, `nfRatingStyle`, `timeBump`, `showVersionText`** — four one-line `Option` rows reading a value the HUD code already computes. Effort: S each.
- **[P2] `badShitBreakCombo`** — Bad/Shit hits count as combo **breaks** instead of misses (SPE `PlayState.hx:3690`). Our combo logic has no such branch. Effort: S.
- **[P2] `guitarHeroSustains`** — sustains are judged as **one note**: the head hit covers the whole hold, sustain segments stop being individually hit-checked (`PlayState.hx:4068` returns early for `parent == null`, `:4090` for sustain notes), and the hold stops paying health per segment (`:4275` sets `gainHealth = false`). Ours judges every segment (`play/PlayState.hx` sustain handling) and pays health per segment. It changes scoring, so it is a *changer*-shaped option, not a cosmetic one — and it makes long holds survivable in dense charts. Effort: M.
- **[P2] `useFlawlessRating` + `flawlessWindow` + `systemScoreMultiplier` ('Psych' | 'Codename').** A **Flawless** judgement above Sick with its own window, and a switchable multi-note **score multiplier/weight table** (300 vs 350 for Sick, weighted 1/0.67/0.34/0). We have `perfect`/`noMarvJudge` and `AccuracyPresets`, but no extra top judgement and no per-judgement score weights. Their `backend/Rating.hx` is the clean 98-line shape to copy: `{name, image, hitWindow, ratingMod, score, noteSplash, hits}` built from prefs. This also cleanly supersedes the "Mic'd Up S/A ranking" wish in §0. Effort: M.
- **[P2] `naughtynessResults`** — flips the Results screen to its "naughty" flavour (`ResultsState.hx:537`). **Already present here** (`grep naughtyness source/` finds it used in our Results screen) — listed only because a naive pref diff flags it. Skip.
- **[P2] `showSustainSplash`/`hideSustainSplash` equivalent.** SPE has a pref that suppresses **sustain** splashes (`objects/SustainSplash.hx:64,85`) and a "Hide Sustain Splash" row in Visuals. We ship `play/objects/SustainSplash` (pooled via `ClientPrefs.maxSplashLimit`, `PlayState.hx:753`) but **no preference to turn it off** — on a hold-heavy phone chart it is pure cost. Effort: S.
- **[P2] Modchart settings block — `camera3dEnabled`, `zScale`, `arrowPaths`, `styledPaths`, `pathBounds`, `optimizeHolds`, `holdsBehindStrum`, `holdEndScale`, `holdCache`, `holdAlphaSteps`, `colMods`, `modchartDebug`.** Their `options/ModchartSettingsSubState.hx` surfaces all twelve, and their renderer reads them (`modchart/backend/graphics/CtxRenderer.hx:183,246`, `renderers/HoldRenderer.hx:160,374`, `renderers/ArrowRenderer.hx:155`). Of these we already expose the equivalent of `camera3dEnabled`+`zScale` as our single `setModchartPerspective` (§12.5) — but **arrow-path rendering** (draw each note's trajectory as a line, with colour/alpha and a pixel bound) and the **hold rendering knobs** (behind-strum, end scale, cached alpha steps, per-lane mods) are genuinely absent: `grep` for `arrowPaths`/`holdsBehindStrum`/`holdEndScale` in `source/` returns nothing on our side. Port behind `ClientPrefs` rows defaulting to their low-risk values (`arrowPaths` off, it is the expensive one). Effort: M for paths, S for the hold knobs.
- **[P2] Per-mod **defined** options — `ModSettingsSubState`.** A mod declares its own `Option` rows in `pack.json` (`settings: [...]`); the Mods menu shows a `SETTINGS` button that opens them and they persist per mod folder. **In Phoenix the whole thing is still commented out** — `states/ModsMenuState.hx:324-336` has the button inside a `// not yet` block, `find source -iname '*ModSettings*'` returns nothing, and the only hit for the name is that dead code. **Re-verified after the `main` merge.** §22.5 has "pre-mod settings *profiles*" (player prefs per mod) which is a **different feature**; this entry is mod-*author*-defined settings. Uncomment, wire `backend.Mods` to read the `settings` array, and keep the existing "no settings" disabled state. Effort: S–M, very high value for modders.
- **[P2] Mobile: `storageType` ('INTERNAL' | 'EXTERNAL_DATA') with `AndroidPermissionsState`, `infinityDisplay`, `showTouchPointer`, `showMobileDebugButtons`, `mobileReceptorAlign`, `controlsAlpha`, `extraButtons`, `hitboxPos`, `hitboxType`, `dynamicColors`.** Our `MobileOptionsSubState` has `mobileCAlpha`, `mobileCEx`, `mobileCExTaunt`, `mobileControlScale`, `androidTVNavigation`, `screensaver`, `hapticFeedback`, `androidNotifications`, `hitboxType`, `hitboxPos` — so we match or beat half of it. Missing: **a storage-location picker with a permission flow** (`android/platform/AndroidStorage.hx` already implements the whole SAF/content-URI layer and there is no UI in front of it — `ClientPrefs` has no `storageType`), a **touch pointer overlay**, a **receptor↔hitbox alignment** toggle, and **dynamic control colours** (pads tint themselves with the song's beat/health colour). These four are one `Option` row each plus wiring. Effort: S–M; the storage UI is the valuable one.
- **[P3] `fullscreenMode` ('Borderless' | 'Borderless Fix' | 'Exclusive') + `instantWindowClose`.** Our §22.2 `windowMode` entry covers the first (three-way Windowed/Borderless/Fullscreen); note SPE distinguishes *two kinds of borderless*, which is the reason people ask for the row at all — fold that distinction into the §22.2 implementation rather than adding a parallel pref.
- **[P3] `windowsGDIEffects` — a consent gate for the GDI/4th-wall payloads.** SPE refuses to run any GDI effect unless the player ticked the box (`options/GameplaySettingsSubState.hx:126`, enforced at `slushithings/windows/WindowsAPI.hx:35`). We own more GDI machinery than they do (`backend/GDIEffects.hx`, `psychlua/callbacks/GdiCallbacks.hx` — 20 functions, `WindowColorMode`) with **no consent gate at all**. Overlaps the "4th-wall effects" toggle in §2.1 — implement once there. Their `changeWindowBorderColorWithNoteHit` and `maxInstanceSlots` are **declared and never read** in SPE (the only reader of the latter is the value's own early loader, `Main.hx:67`), so ignore both. Effort: S.

**Declared in SPE but never read — do NOT port as options:** `heyIntro` (**the row exists at `options/VisualsSettingsSubState.hx:237` and nothing ever reads `ClientPrefs.data.heyIntro`**), `maxInstanceSlots`, `changeWindowBorderColorWithNoteHit`, `holdCache`, `popUpRating` (six reads, all UI-local), `gameOverVibration` (only their `GameOverSubstate`). Every entry above that I did *not* mark dead was confirmed at a real read site; keep that habit when porting, because SPE's `SaveVariables` class is wider than its behaviour.

---

### 32.4. GAMEPLAY, HUD & CHART EVENTS

- **[P1] `Change Stage` chart event (instant, zero-drop).** SPE's `backend/StageChanger.hx` (621 lines) reconstitutes a stage **as plain `FlxSprite`s, never instantiating the stage classes** (which is what avoids the classic `Limo.update()` null crash), queues and **preloads every stage the chart will use onto a hidden slot**, then swaps on the event's beat with a blackout/char/cam kill-switch set (`noblack | nochars | nozoom | nocam | nogf`).
