We do **not** have this: `grep -rn "'Change Stage'" source/` finds exactly one hit, in `states/LoadingState.hx:329`, which merely *reads* the event to collect characters for preloading — **the event does nothing at runtime**. This is a concrete, documented feature with a known-hard problem already solved in the source. Port with credits. Effort: M.
- **[P2] `Lyric Event`.** Value 1 = text, shown in a tweened `FlxText` at 72% height, empty clears it — the widget is built lazily only when the event is queued (`PlayState.hx:266` field, `:601-606` creation, `:2990` case). We have no lyrics anywhere. Effort: S. Pairs with §23.3's proposed `Add Subtitle` (same widget, so build the widget once).
- **[P2] `Hurt Note` as a **chart event**. The note **type** already works here: `'Hurt Note'` is in our chart-editor list (`editors/ChartingState.hx:76`), the texture swap is `objects/Note.hx:673` with `noteSplashes/HURTnoteSplashes`, and the editor preview already applies `missHealth 0.3` / `hitCausesMiss` (`editors/helpers/EditorPlayStateHelpers.hx:105`). What is missing is the **event form** that SPE adds at `PlayState.hx:4285` — spawn hurt notes from an event timeline instead of authoring each note by hand. Small, and it reuses the type above. Effort: S.
- **[P3] `Set Camera Bopping`, `Add Camera Zoom`, `Camera Follow Pos`, `Set GF Speed`, `Kill Henchmen`, `Play Animation`, `Play Sound`, `Alt Idle Animation` — all already here** (`play/helpers/PlayStateEvents.hx` accepts every one of these names). Listed so nobody re-ports them.
- **[P3] "Character JSON Generator (Anims from XML)"** — the character-editor feature in §32.2; listed here because their README pitches it as a headline and it is *not* a content feature.

---

### 32.5. MODCHART ENGINE

Their `source/modchart/` is upstream Plus Engine (§5.3, ~60 modifiers, macro registry, event timeline, Lua/HScript modifiers, **standalone adapter**), and ours is a smaller native port (35 registered names in `modchart/ModifierRegistry.hx:56-90`). Concretely:

- **[P2] Twelve named modifiers with no equivalent here.** Verified by name against `ModifierRegistry`: `Asymptote`, `Attenuate`, `Carousel`, `Cubic`, `Digital`, `Field`, `LuaPath`, `ModSpline`, `Orient`, `Parabola`, `RandomSpeed`, `StraightHolds`, plus the `false_paradise/` set (`EyeShape`, `SchmovinArrowShape` — we do have `Spiral`, `Vibrate`, `Wiggle`, `CounterClockWise`) and `psych_noteTween/` (`NoteTweenAngle`, `NoteTweenDirection` — we have the `noteTween*` **Lua functions** but not as modchart mods). `PathModifier` and `ArrowShape` are already tracked in §12.5/§23.1 — cross-referenced, not duplicated. The rest are small offset/curve math on the same `Modifier` base; port as a batch. Effort: M total.
- **[P2] Raw modifier access + scheduled-easing API — `setRawValue`/`getRawValue`, `addNow`, `easeNow`, `callback`, `repeater`, `scheduleCallback`, `queueSet/queueEase/queueFunc/queueFuncOnce` (+`*P` variants), `alias`, `node`, `quickRegister`, `getVisPos`/`getBaseVisPosD`, `getBaseX/Y`, `getArrowSize(Div2)`, `getHoldSize(Div2)`, `getHoldSubdivisions`/`setHoldSubdivisions`, `setModifierPath(Bound/Offset)`, `createModifier`/`createLuaModifier`/`addScriptedModifier`, `removePlayfield`, `getPlayerCount`.** We expose exactly **14** modchart functions (`callbacks/ModchartCallbacks.hx`: `addModifier, addPlayfield, getModchartModifiers, getPercent, isModchartActive, modAdd, modCallback, modEase, modRepeater, modSet, modSetAdd, resetModchart, setModchartPerspective, setPercent`). Those 14 are 1:1 with our manager API (`ModchartManager.hx` public methods) — i.e. **every gap above is also a gap in the engine layer**, not just the binding. Notably missing capabilities: *script-authored modifiers at runtime* (`createLuaModifier` — a big one for NotITG-style mod packs), naming playfields/`alias`es for multi-field charts, scheduled callbacks by beat, and any read of the post-transform visual position. Port in that order. Effort: L; split it — `alias` + `removePlayfield` + read-side getters first (S), scripted modifiers after (M), path/ease queue last (M).
- **[P3] Standalone adapter.** Their `modchart/backend/standalone/` runs the whole modchart engine with a `IAdapter`/`Adapter` pair and a `psych` implementation, so a preview or an external tool can drive it with no `PlayState`. Our `ModchartLabState` is the in-engine equivalent and already does exactly this (§21.1, DONE) — so this is **not** a gap. Recorded so it is not re-reviewed.

---

### 32.6. UI KIT & THEMING

- **[P2] Material Design 3 widget set (`backend/ui/md3/`, 12 files) + `OptionsMenuTheme` (365) + `ThemeAccentColorSubState` (586).** Buttons, boxes, chips, checkboxes, text fields, linear/wavy progress rings, loading indicator, a `NetworkCheckToast`, plus `MD3Theme`/`MD3Metrics`/`MD3ShapeTools` that derive a *full tonal palette* (primary/secondary/container/surface roles) from one accent colour, in light and dark. Our `objects/ui/CustomFlxUI.hx` is **243 lines** of FlxUI subclasses plus string-preset styling (`backend/IconStyle`, `scoreStyle`, `timeBarStyle`) — there is **no theme system and no accent-colour picker** (`grep -rn 'accentColor' source/` → nothing). Their `ThemeAccentColorSubState` even has HEX copy/paste and a live widget preview. This is the UI layer behind every screen they modernised, and it is the biggest *visual* gap in the review. Start with `MD3Theme` + `MaterialButton`/`MaterialTextField`/`MaterialWavyProgressIndicator` (that last one is what the Break Timer uses) rather than porting all twelve. Effort: L; `MD3Theme` alone (S) unlocks the accent-colour option.
- **[P3] `objects/Cursor.hx` — a global cursor abstraction.** `Cursor.show/hide/toggle/set(CursorMode)/loadCustom`, cursor-mode **names** (`Default`, etc.) with a cache of custom BitmapData, wired into both the Plus and the V-Slice runtimes. We have per-state `FlxG.mouse.visible = true` scattered around plus `backend.PointingCursorLogic` for the menu finger — no named cursor modes and no custom-cursor loader. Their file is small and the official Funkin cursor art is what it uses. Effort: S.

---

### 32.7. INTERNATIONALISATION ⭐

- **[P1] A real translation layer — 12 hardcoded language classes + a `.lang` fallback + an in-game language picker.** `backend/languages/` ships `EnUS, EsLA, EsES, PtBR, FrFR, DeDE, ItIT, NlNL, IdID, JpJP, ZhCN, ZhHK, RuRUS` (7,165 lines; **476 keys** in `EnUS` alone, incl. per-option `name_*`/`description_*` keys), loaded by `backend/Language.hx` with a phrase cache, a merged-mod-override path (`data/<locale>.lang`), `Language.getPhrase(key, fallback)` everywhere, a `LanguageSubState` picker, Android auto-detecting the device locale on first run, and `getFileTranslation`/`getTranslationPhrase` **also exposed to Lua** so mods can translate their own content. **Phoenix has nothing**: `grep -rn 'getPhrase' source/` → 0 hits, and the only trace of i18n is `ClientPrefs.locale = 'en'` (`ClientPrefs.hx:63`, **written and never read**) and one line in §12.4 (`TODO.md:234`, P3: *"Localization / translations. No language key system or locale files."*). This is under-prioritised at P3: Phx has a large Brazilian and Latin-American audience (the TODO itself is written with BR/LATAM idioms), and the whole system is *mechanical* to port — keys are already isolated in `Alphabet`/`Option` labels, and their language files are Apache-2.0 with named authors (`Hogich` for RU). Do it in two passes: **(a)** the `Language` loader + `getPhrase` + `LanguageSubState` + `en-US` only (S), **(b)** port the eleven other files as data (M, mostly transcription). Keep all thirteen, credit each translator in `CreditsState`. Effort: **M**, and it is the highest-value *non-editor* item in this review.

---

### 32.8. PLATFORM / NATIVE

- **[P1] Android **storage location** support (scoped vs public) with a permission state.** `mobile/backend/StorageUtil.hx` (898 lines of JNI) + `mobile/states/AndroidPermissionsState.hx` + a `storageType` pref, handling SDK-version differences, creating directories, **copying the user's existing `mods/` and `sm/` folders when the mode changes**, and a status string for the settings screen. We have a large native Android layer (`android/platform/AndroidStorage.hx` with the full SAF/`content://` implementation, `AndroidIntents`, `AndroidNotification`, `AndroidBridge`) — **more capable at the native level than theirs** — but nothing in Options selects a storage root and no migration copies mods. Ties into §11. Effort: M.
- **[P2] `backend/ShaderCompatibility.hx` — runtime shader *dialect* rewriting.** At runtime it parses incoming GLSL and: strips uniform initialisers, rewrites `mainImage`/`iResolution` Shadertoy entry points into `main`, **localises OpenFL global initialisers**, warns on unsupported uniforms, normalises strict constructors, and adapts **`notitg:`- and `shadertoy:`-flagged** sources. This is what makes 0.6.3-era and Shadertoy-pasted mod shaders compile without the modder touching them. Our `shaders/RuntimeShaders.hx` is a hardcoded enum of *engine* shaders and `ErrorHandledShader` only catches errors — we do **not** rewrite third-party GLSL. Given our far larger shader catalogue and §23.2's ports, this is the missing compatibility half. Port `adaptRuntimeShaderCode` + the two dialect adapters. Effort: M (700 lines, mostly regex + string work).
- **[P3] `backend/ClassResolver.hx` — old-Psych class-path compatibility map.** 371 lines of `'old.path' => 'new.path'` covering Psych **0.6.3 → 0.7.3 → 1.0.4 → PlusEngine**, so a script's `import funkin.objects.Note` or `funkin.backend.ClientPrefs` still resolves. We renamed a lot (`play/`, `psychlua/callbacks/`, `data/`) and ship **no alias table**, so old HScript mods fail at import. `docs/research/psych-fork-engine-changes.md` shows this is a fork-wide problem; ours is the most refactored tree of the set, so we need it most. Also add their `LEGACY_PREF_NAMES` pattern (`ClientPrefs.hx:196`) — a name-migration table for renamed prefs (`showEarlyLateSprites`→`showTimingTags`, etc.), which we currently handle ad hoc or not at all. Effort: M.
- **[P3] `lime/media/AudioManager.hx` + `alsoft.txt` → `plugins/alsoft.ini|conf`.** They shadow `AudioManager` to apply an OpenAL-Soft config on mobile (where it cannot be dropped next to the exe) and ship `alsoft` with HRTF/period settings. We already read the ALSoft version (`Native.getOpenALSoftVersion`) and have an `alsoft` string in `project.hxp` — worth checking whether our shipped config actually reaches Android. Effort: S, verification first.
- **[P3] `backend/RenderInterpolation.hx` + `PsychAnimationController`.** Frame-time interpolation of camera positions between fixed steps, and an animation controller that scales with a global `FlxG.animationTimeScale` (so animations keep real-world speed at 30/60/240 fps). We already set `FlxG.animationTimeScale` (`PlayState.hx:641`) and patch `FlxGame._accumulator` (`source-haxelib-patches/flixel/FlxGame.hx:589`), so **the interpolation half may already exist** — verify before porting; if not, their 69-line `RenderInterpolation` is the reference. Effort: S.
- **[P3] Build/asset ergonomics worth copying:** `assets/base_game/` + `assets/week_assets/` + `assets/translations/` split with `rename=` in `Project.xml` (so a translated asset set overlays by path, not by code), `backend/AssetLoader`/`AssetCache`/`AssetResolver`/`TextureOptimizer`/`ThreadUtil` (the last two explicitly "for low-end devices, does NOT resize textures — that breaks Sparrow XML coordinates" — the exact trap our §0 "compress all images" item would fall into), and `BuildInfo`/`BuildInfoGenerated` for an in-game build stamp. Our `backend.CommitFreshness`/`HaxeCommit` covers the stamp; the **`TextureOptimizer` warning is the valuable part** — record it next to the §0 downscale goal. Effort: S to document, M to implement.

---

### 32.9. GAME CONTENT SHIPPED

- **[P3] Two extra base-game songs: `ridge` and `smash`.** `assets/base_game/shared/data/{ridge,smash}/` (chart + `song_meta.json`, 28 KB each) — chart/editor test songs (both are empty-section skeletons in the tree), plus `song_meta.json` for all 29 songs. We ship 29 song folders in `assets/preload/data/` but **no `song_meta.json` anywhere** and no `ridge`/`smash`. Content itself is trivial; what matters is the **`song_meta.json` schema** — see §32.10.
- **[P3] Note skins & HUD art:** `NOTE_assets-notitg` (a fifth note skin), `HURTNOTE_assets` + `HURTnoteSplashes` (we ship these — see §32.4), `holdCovers/` (their hold-splash art set; ours is `noteSplashes/holdSplashes/holdSplash*` per `play/objects/SustainSplash.hx:33`, so nothing is dangling here — cited only as art they ship and we do not), `comboBroken.png` + `pixelUI/comboBroken-pixel.png` (a **combo-broken** popup, absent here), `albumRoll/` + `Menu_Tracks.png` variants, `early.png`/`late.png`/`flawless.png` (we have early/late/perfect via `ratings/<style>/`), `miss.png`, `controllertype.png`, and per-locale image overrides under `assets/translations/shared/images/<locale>/`. The two worth adding to our asset set are the **note-skin #5** and **`comboBroken`**; the locale-override folder layout only matters with §32.7.
- **[P3] Week-1..7 stage polish** (BF/GF "Hey!" reactions on specific songs, Tank lightning flash, henchmen death anims, Week 6 HUD-hide during the cutscene, slower GF head-bop). Song-specific tuning rather than engine work; port only if we want FNF-vanilla parity marketing. Not a TODO item beyond a note.

---

### 32.10. DATA FORMATS

- **[P2] `song_meta.json` per song, with a real schema.** `{coverAlbum, albumId, card, songAuthor, artist, composer, freeplayWeekName, freeplaySongLength, freeplayPrevStart, freeplayPrevEnd, links{newgrounds,bandcamp,youtube,x}, licenses[], licenseText}` — read by `FreeplayState.hx:2703-2720` (album art, author, preview window, license badges, social links) and by the chart editor's Meta tab (`ChartingState.hx:4407`). This is the data behind their whole V-Slice-style Freeplay. Ours has **no meta file**: `assets/preload/data/<song>/` holds only chart JSONs + `notetypes.txt`, so author/preview-window/license/link data has nowhere to live. Cross-references §12.2's `meta.json` entry (CNE) — **implement both from one schema**, and keep it optional so existing mods load unchanged. Effort: M.
- **[P2] Consume the stage `preload` map at load time.** `data/StageData.hx:36-40` now declares `objects`, `preload` and `_editorMeta`, the Stage Editor writes them (`data/StageEditorCore.hx:299`, `editors/helpers/StageEditorHelpers.hx:164`), and chat loading calls `buildPreloadQueue()` (`states/LoadingState.hx:164`) — but **nothing reads `stage.preload` to warm those images/sounds** (`grep -rn '\.preload' source/` finds only the writers). So the manifest round-trips and does nothing. The read side is what SPE's `PreloadListSubState` + loader pair provides: `{'images/spraycanAtlas/': 3}`, where the number is a quality/keep-alive class. Also missing: the in-editor list UI for it. Effort: M.
- **[P3] `psych_v2` chart format + editor support (`Song.hx:395,531` auto-detect and convert `format:"psych_v2"` → v1, flat note/event/BPM arrays instead of sections) and the V-Slice import button (`states/editors/content/VSlice.hx` 490 lines, `PsychJsonPrinter` 220, plus `VSliceFreeplayState`).** Our `data/Song.hx:256-264` **already** handles `format` / psych-v1 (`psychV1Chart` flag, strips `format`, returns the object), so psych_v1 is covered — **psych_v2 and the V-Slice *import path*** are not, and V-Slice charts are what modern mods ship. Note §12.2 asks for CNE's `meta.json`+`charts/<diff>.json` layout; a V-Slice importer is the same work seen from the other side. Effort: M–L.

---

### 32.11. ARCHITECTURE IDEAS WORTH TAKING

- **[P2] Scripted classes with real native superclasses (`psychlua/backend/ScriptedClass.hx` 361 + `ScriptedNativeFactory.hx` 670).** An HScript file can declare `class MySprite extends FlxSprite { … }` and get an actual `FlxSprite`-compatible instance, with `FlxSprite`, `FlxSpriteGroup`, `FlxState`, `FlxSubState`, `MusicBeatState`, `MusicBeatSubstate` as supported parents, `IScriptCustomConstructor`/`hnew`, per-instance interpreters seeded from the parent's globals, and `IScriptSuperProxyProvider` for `super` calls. We build on `hscript-improved` (`hmm.json`), which *does* have a `ScriptedClass`-adjacent `ClassDecl` code path — but we register no supported-parent factory, no per-instance interpreter seeding and no `super` proxy, so a script class cannot extend a native sprite in practice. There is **no `addScriptedClass`-style surface anywhere in our tree** — a script cannot define a type, so `new` of a script-declared class is impossible and `addScriptedClass`-style mods cannot run. This is why SPE runs mods we cannot. Cross-reference §12.1's "HScript module system". Both trees now sit on `hscript-improved`, so this is **not a dependency swap** — it is the missing factory/seeding layer. Effort: L, and it is the deepest change in this review.
- **[P3] Macro helpers — `ScriptClassExportMacro`, `ScriptedBridgeMacro`, `PlusFlxMacro`.** Compile-time generation of HScript-visible wrappers around Haxe classes, so "expose this class to scripts" is an annotation rather than hand-written glue. Interesting, but it depends on §32.11's first entry. Record only.
- **[P3] Gameplay-changers as a **collection** with a public API — `addGameplayOption` / `removeGameplayOption` / `getOptionByName` / `getCurrentOption` / `selectOption(index)` / `rebuildOptionsVisuals()` + six companion callbacks (`onGameplayChangerCreatePost/Rebuild/SelectionChange/Reset/Accept/Back`).** Our `GameplayChangersHelpers` builds a fixed list. Note **their list is not better than ours** — they have 14 changers, we have 20+ including `bothsides`, `flip`, `onekey`, `jacks`, `randommode`, `stairmode`, `wavemode`, `thetrollingever`, and most of theirs (`opponentplay`, `opponentdrain`, `nodroppenalty`, `perfect`, `botplayType`) are **registered but never read in their `PlayState`** (`grep` finds only the option rows). Port the **API shape**, not the changers. Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] `objects/GlobalLoadingOverlay` — a persistent Material spinner usable from anywhere.** A static `show/pulse/showPersistent/hide` overlay in the `openfl.display` layer, above every Flx state. Our `states.LoadingState` + `LoadingPieDial` are per-state. Useful for the mod-import, reload and chart-load paths. Effort: S.
- **[P3] `TransitionManager` + `CustomFadeTransition` as a **scriptable** transition (`ScriptableSubstate.tryCreate('TransitionSubstate')`), with door/gradient/watermark/event-text/icon art and `cancelCurrentTransition()`.** Ours (`shaders/CustomFadeTransition.hx`) is a bare gradient + black bar and is not replaceable by a script. Replace-the-transition is a popular mod ask; tie it to §12.1's "scripted transitions". Effort: M.

---

### 32.12. CHECKED AND **NOT** WORTH PORTING

- **`2hot`-era song list and weeks.** Same as ours; `ridge`/`smash` are empty test charts (§32.9).
- **Their `AccuracyPresets`-less rating table.** Their `backend/Rating.hx` is a *nice shape* (§32.3 Flawless entry) but it has no preset system at all — ours (`backend/AccuracyPresets.hx`, `Vanilla/Psych/Kade/ITG/Custom`) is strictly ahead. Do not replace ours.
- **`backend/MemoryManager.hx` (458) / `backend/Cache.hx` / `AssetCache`.** Our `backend.AssetCache`, `MemoryLedger`, `MemoryProfiles`, `LowMemory`, `PoolStats`, `Paths.clearUnusedMemory` stack is broader and tested (`tests/haxe/*Memory*`). Skip.
- **`debug/TraceDisplay.hx` (675) + `TraceButton`.** An in-game coloured trace console. Ours routes traces to `VLog` + `DebugLuaText` + the crash bundle; an on-screen console is §12.3's ImGui Console entry, which is a better target.
- **`debug/FPSCounter` FPS "modes"** (Visible with/without Background, Basic/Extended Debug). We have `showFPS`, `legacyFPSUI`, `show1PercentLows`, `showRamUsage/Graph`, `debugInfo`, `FrameProfiler` — a superset. Skip.
- **`objects/MusicPlayer` / `MusicPlayerPsych` preview players.** We have `music/MusicPlayer.hx` wired into `FreeplayState.hx:352` plus the auto-preview entry in §22.5. Skip.
- **`objects/Watermark`, `objects/SustainSplash`, `objects/TypedAlphabet`, `objects/CheckboxThingie`, `objects/AttachedSprite/Text`, `objects/Alphabet`, `HealthIcon`, `MenuCharacter`.** All present here under the same names. Skip.
- **`objects/StickerSubState`** (a sticker-board transition from a `stickerPack` JSON, `FlxTransitionableState` based). Fun, purely cosmetic, no consumer in their tree. Skip.
- **`states/FreeplayState_Psych` / `VSliceFreeplayState` / `FreeplayStateSelector`.** Our `FreeplayState` already has a filter page, search, favourites, stars and per-mod filtering (§21.2 DONE) — richer than their legacy-Psych fallback. Only the **selector indirection** is worth taking, for §32.2's menu scripting.
- **`substates/OutdatedSubState` rework / `backend/Discord` (old RPC) / `backend/Achievements` placement.** Ours is newer (`backend/UpdateChecker`, `DiscordClient`, `AchievementsMenuState` + score API). Skip.
- **Their Lua API *wrappers* that duplicate ours under another name:** `doTweenX/Y/Angle/Alpha/Zoom/Color`, `noteTween*`, `startTween`/`cancelTween`, `runTimer`/`cancelTimer`, `mouseClicked/Pressed/Released`, `keyboardJustPressed/Pressed/Released`, `anyGamepad*`, `gamepadAnalogX/Y`, `getRandomInt/Float/Bool`, `stringStartsWith/EndsWith`, `getProperty*`/`setProperty*`/`getObjectOrder`/`setObjectOrder`, `setHealthBarColors`/`setTimeBarColors`/`setObjectCamera`/`screenCenter`/`objectsOverlap`/`getPixelColor`, `makeLuaSprite`/`makeAnimatedLuaSprite`/`addAnimation*`/`playAnim`/`scaleObject`/`updateHitbox`, `makeLuaText`/`setText*`, `playMusic`/`playSound`/`fade*`/`getSoundVolume`…, `loadGraphic`/`loadFrames`, `precacheImage/Sound/Music`, `addLuaScript`/`removeLuaScript`/`addHScript`, `startDialogue`/`startVideo`, `cameraSetTarget`/`cameraShake`/`cameraFlash`/`cameraFade`, `setRatingPercent`/`setRatingName`/`setRatingFC`, `getMouseX/Y`, `characterDance`, `triggerEvent`, `startCountdown`/`endSong`/`restartSong`/`exitSong`/`getSongPosition`, `addCharacterToList`, `getScore`/`getMisses`/`getHits`/`addScore`/`addMisses`/`addHits`/`setScore`/`setMisses`/`setHits`/`setHealth`/`addHealth`/`getHealth`, the `luaSprite*`/`objectPlayAnimation` deprecated aliases, `getRunningScripts`/`callScript`/`isRunning`, `FlxColor`, `getColorFromHex`, `debugPrint`, `getMidpointX/Y`, `getScreenPositionX/Y`. **All present here** — verified one by one. Every one of these was in my initial 253-name diff and is a false positive from the `add_callback` vs `registerFunction` naming split; they are listed here so the next reviewer does not re-diff by hand.
- **Their `addAnimationByIndicesLoop` deprecated alias.** We have `addAnimationByIndicesLoop` (`callbacks/SpriteCallbacks.hx`). Skip.
- **Meme/joke content:** `assets/secrets/` Pessy/Shadow/River bumpers + jingles, `menuThemeMode` joke presets, `curses`/"Little Timmy Mode". Some of these are *nice* (we have `ClothEasterEgg`/`MetaHorror` in the same spirit) but they are their engine's mascot; not portable content.
- **Two behaviour changes that would be regressions here:** their `BeatTimerHud`-style timer lives inside `PlayState`'s `update` (per-frame string formatting — ours would go in `play/helpers/`); and `HudEditorState` hardcodes 4K strums **and** states in its own header that icons cannot be moved because `PlayState` rewrites them every frame — if we ever generalise lane count (§25.1), the ported HUD editor must not inherit that constraint.

---

### 32.13. SUGGESTED ORDER (highest value ÷ effort first)

1. **Free wins, one day each:** `Hurt Note` event (§32.4), `Lyric Event` (§32.4), the missing single-key `Option` rows — `dynamicCombo`, `nfRatingStyle`, `timeBump`, `showVersionText`, sustain-splash toggle, `badShitBreakCombo` (§32.3), `addToGroup`, `getColorFromString/Name`, `updateScoreText`, `getSoundPitch/setSoundPitch`, camera-scroll getters/setters (§32.1), and a `comboBroken` popup (§32.9).
2. **Scripting parity pass:** script-local variables, state introspection, mobile bindings, clipboard/notification/screenshot bindings, `getChartNotes` (§32.1). Then `WindowTweens` + window geometry (§32.1.1) — transcription, big payoff for desktop mods.
3. **i18n pass (a):** `backend.Language` + `getPhrase` + picker + `en-US` (§32.7). Then (b) the other twelve files as data.
4. **`Change Stage` event + `StageChanger`** (§32.4) and **`ModSettingsSubState` uncomment** (§32.3) — both are already-written code sitting unused in one tree or the other.
5. **Chart editor Edit/View tabs** (copy/cut/paste/select-all/grouped undo/section jump/waveform target) + **MIDI import** + **`song_meta.json` + Meta tab** (§32.2, §32.10).
6. **`ShaderCompatibility`** (§32.8) — unlocks every legacy mod shader at once.
7. **`HUD Editor`** (§32.2) — the single biggest feature in this review; do it after step 5 so it can reuse the editor's undo/selection work.
8. **Scripted classes** (§32.11) — a dependency decision as much as a port; scope it separately.
9. **Material 3 theme + accent colour** (§32.6) and **Android storage location** (§32.8).
10. **Modchart modifier batch + scripted modifiers** (§32.5), **Cover Maker** and **Event Creator** (§32.2) as the long tail.

Nothing in this section should land without an entry in `docs/` (+ `website/docs/` where it is a subsystem), a `tests/haxe/run-*.sh` spec for the pure-logic half, and per-feature credits as the rule at the top of this file requires.

---

## 33. CANDIDATE HAXELIBS & ECOSYSTEM DEPENDENCIES (2026-11-21)

Audited against Phoenix Engine's current `hmm.json` (20 dependencies), `project.hxp`, `SIZE.md`, **Apache-2.0** license requirements (MIT / BSD / Apache-2.0 only), and single-branch multi-target builds (`cpp` Desktop + Android/iOS + `html5`). Overlaps with §0–§32 are cross-referenced rather than duplicated.

### 33.1. High-Payoff Roadmap Libraries

- **[P1] `moonchart` ([`MaybeMaru/moonchart`](https://github.com/MaybeMaru/moonchart) — MIT, pure Haxe, all targets).** Converts rhythm-game charts across **FNF** (Legacy, Psych, V-Slice, Codename), **osu!mania** (`.osu`/`.osz`), **StepMania** (`.sm`/`.ssc`), **Quaver** (`.qua`/`.qp`), Guitar Hero (`.chart`), and MIDI (`.mid`). Directly unlocks cross-game import/export in `ChartingState` and CNE/V-Slice chart loading (§12.2, §31.4, §32.10) with zero native NDLL overhead. Note Frozen Engine's `ChartConvertCore.hx` fixes for >7K lane wrapping and `offsetHolds` (`docs/research/psych-fork-engine-changes.md` §5.4). Effort: M.
- **[P2] `rulescript` ([`Kriptel/RuleScript`](https://github.com/Kriptel/RuleScript) — MIT) or `hscript-iris` ([`crowplexus/hscript-iris`](https://github.com/crowplexus/hscript-iris) — MIT, pure Haxe, all targets).** Modern HScript upgrades over `CodenameCrew/hscript-improved` adding scripted `class`/`enum` declarations, `import ... as`, `using`, package/module sharing, string interpolation, and stricter `#if` preprocessor support — advancing §12.1 (*HScript module system*) and §32.11 (*Scripted classes*). Keep all reflection/imports gated through `ModSecurity` and `ScriptSecurityScanner`. Effort: M–L.
- **[P2] `hxgamejolt-api` ([`MAJigsaw77/hxgamejolt-api`](https://github.com/MAJigsaw77/hxgamejolt-api) — MIT, pure Haxe, all targets).** Actively maintained GameJolt API bindings by MAJigsaw77 (same maintainer as Phoenix's `hxdiscord_rpc`, `hxnativefiledialog`, and `hxgamemode`), replacing the legacy Screwed Engine `GJClient.hx` snippet (§0) and unlicensed/unmaintained `tentools` (§27.8). Effort: S–M.
- **[P2] `flxgif` ([`MAJigsaw77/flxgif`](https://github.com/MAJigsaw77/flxgif) — MIT, all targets).** Animated `.gif` playback directly on Flixel sprites. Pairs with the planned in-game GameBanana mod browser (§0, §31.3) so mod thumbnails, avatars, and banners render without converting GIFs to Sparrow atlases. Effort: S.
- **[P2] `FunkinModchart` ([`TheoDevelops/FunkinModchart`](https://github.com/TheoDevelops/FunkinModchart) — Apache-2.0, Flixel `cpp`/`html5`).** Implements true 3D arrow meshes and subdivided sustain-strip triangle rendering (`FlxDrawTrianglesItem`) for HaxeFlixel VSRGs. Either adopt directly or use as an Apache-2.0 reference for `modchart/SustainStripGeom.hx` so bent/reversed modifiers in `source/modchart/` no longer detach rigid sustain sprites (§28). Effort: M–L.

### 33.2. UI, Editors, Localization & Vector Assets

- **[P2] `haxeui-core` + `haxeui-flixel` ([`haxeui/haxeui-core`](https://github.com/haxeui/haxeui-core), [`haxeui/haxeui-flixel`](https://github.com/haxeui/haxeui-flixel) — MIT, all targets).** `project.hxp` (`configureCompileDefines`) already defines `haxeui_no_mouse_reset`, `haxeui_focus_out_on_click`, and `haxeui_dont_impose_base_class` without the library installed. Provides dockable panels, tree views, rich tooltips (§0), color pickers, and scroll containers for `StageEditorState`, `ChartingState`, and `ModchartLabState`, reducing reliance on `flixel-ui 2.6.0` (which currently requires 5 overrides under `source/source-haxelib-patches/flixel/addons/ui/`). Effort: L.
- **[P2] `firetongue` ([`larsiusprime/firetongue`](https://github.com/larsiusprime/firetongue) — MIT) or `kotoba` ([`MAJigsaw77/kotoba`](https://github.com/MAJigsaw77/kotoba) — MIT, pure Haxe, all targets).** Localization/i18n frameworks (§12.4, §31.2, §32.7). `firetongue` provides TSV/CSV/JSON translation tables, pluralization, and **per-locale font replacement rules** (pairing naturally with `AlphabetFont` and §0's custom game-wide font goal); `kotoba` offers a lightweight XML-based alternative if minimal footprint is preferred. Effort: M.
- **[P3] `svg` ([`openfl/svg`](https://github.com/openfl/svg) — MIT, pure Haxe, all targets).** Rasterizes vector `.svg` assets into `BitmapData` at runtime at the exact target DPI (`backend/ScreenDensity.hx`, widescreen `16:10` / `9:17`), keeping UI/editor icons crisp across 4K desktop and mobile without inflating APK/ZIP asset size (`SIZE.md`). Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] `hxluau` ([`ShadowEngineTeam/hxluau`](https://github.com/ShadowEngineTeam/hxluau) — MIT, `cpp`).** Optional Luau scripting backend alongside `hxluajit` for built-in VM sandboxing, faster cold start on JIT-restricted targets (iOS), and optional typed Lua scripts. Phoenix's `FunkinCrew/hxcpp` C++17 + `ml64` toolchain setup in `BUILDING.md` already documents Luau compatibility. Effort: L.

### 33.3. Performance, Binary Formats, Networking & Testing

- **[P2] `json2object` ([`elnabo/json2object`](https://github.com/elnabo/json2object) — MIT, compile-time macros, all targets).** Generates reflection-free, type-safe JSON parsers and writers at compile time with line/column schema validation errors. Ideal for `SwagSong`, `CharacterFile`, `StageData`, and `WeekData` — cutting chart parse/reflection overhead (§8) and safely catching malformed mod JSONs (§6) even with `-dce no`. Effort: M.
- **[P2] `hxbit` ([`HeapsIO/hxbit`](https://github.com/HeapsIO/hxbit) — BSD, pure Haxe, all targets).** Fast, compact macro-driven binary serialization. Fits frame-accurate **Replays** (§14, `phoenix://replay/<id>` in §11) and binary chart caching (§26) without platform-specific C++ `mmap` glue. Effort: M.
- **[P3] `hxWebSockets` ([`ianharrigan/hxWebSockets`](https://github.com/ianharrigan/hxWebSockets) — MIT) & `colyseus` ([`colyseus/colyseus-hx`](https://github.com/colyseus/colyseus-hx) — MIT, `cpp`/`html5`).** Cross-platform WebSockets and multiplayer room state synchronization across Desktop, Android/iOS, and `html5` if opt-in online multiplayer or live spectator rooms (§31.3) are built. Effort: L (with online feature work).
- **[P3] `hxdr_libs` ([`MAJigsaw77/hxdr_libs`](https://github.com/MAJigsaw77/hxdr_libs) — MIT, `cpp`).** Single-header native `dr_wav` / `dr_flac` / `dr_mp3` audio decoders for Lime, handy for importing `.wav`/`.flac`/`.mp3` audio directly into desktop editors and chart converters (§31.4). Effort: S–M.
- **[P3] `utest` ([`haxe-utest/utest`](https://github.com/haxe-utest/utest) — MIT, all targets incl. `--interp`).** Lightweight unit/async assertion and fixture runner compatible with `haxe --interp`, complementing Phoenix's Haxe-only engine spec requirement (`tests/haxe/*Spec.hx` in `AGENTS.md` §7). Effort: S.

### 33.4. Libraries Explicitly Ruled Out & Integration Rules

- **Skip `polymod` ([`larsiusprime/polymod`](https://github.com/larsiusprime/polymod)):** Overlaps Phoenix's `Mods` + `Paths` + `ModZipInstaller` + `PlatformSupport` pipeline; implement the narrow `_polymod_meta.json` folder importer from §29 instead of hooking OpenFL asset libraries.
- **Skip `extension-androidtools` / `hxCodec` / `extension-webm`:** Redundant with `quack.fnf.phoenix.android.*` Java/JNI extensions (`source/android/`) and `hxvlc`.
- **Integration checklist (`AGENTS.md` §8 rule 7):** Every added library must be (1) pinned in `hmm.json`, (2) added across all three setup script dialects (`setup/windows.{bat,ps1,sh}` and `setup/unix.{sh,ps1,bat}`) so `python3 tools/check_dependencies.py` passes, and (3) gated in `project.hxp` (`configureHaxelibs()`) behind a `FeatureFlag` (and `isCPP()` when native-only) so `html5` and compact CI builds keep compiling cleanly.


---

## 34. VS ROB: REBOOTED — ENGINE REUSE REVIEW (`Joalor64/VS-Rob-Rebooted`, 2026-11-21)

**Source:** <https://github.com/Joalor64/VS-Rob-Rebooted> — 100 commits, HEAD `a5bf678` (pushed 2025-06-24), 80 Haxe files + assets. It is a **mod**, not an engine: `Project.xml` still says `version="0.2.7"`, `MainMenuState.psychEngineVersion = '0.5.2h'`, and the README credits "Psych Engine Team (0.5.2h)". So "what the engine has" = **Psych Engine 0.5.2h + Joalor64's own additions on top**.

**Method:** cloned the repo, then diffed its `source/` against a clone of Psych Engine tag `0.5.2h` (`diff -rq` + per-file diffs) to separate *upstream Psych* from *Joalor64's changes*, then checked every Joalor64 change against this tree (grep + read). Also extracted and diffed the Lua callback table, the Lua `set()` globals, the `callOnLuas`/`setOnLuas` hook tables and the HScript `setVariable` table against Phoenix's `psychlua/` registration. Nothing was built or run.

**License — read this before porting anything.** The repo `LICENSE` is **not Apache 2.0** and not an OSI licence: "If you want to modify the code and assets yourself or if you want to make a mod with my engine, OPEN-SOURCE YOUR PROJECT because if you don't, I will happily ask you to take down the project. AND you have to send me $300000000 worth of bitcoin. >:) / Okay the last one was a joke. But still, please open-source your mod. :) / Also, credit isn't necessary, but would be nice. / Copyright 2021-2025 Joalor64 / Upstream (ShadowMario/FNF-PsychEngine): Copyright 2021-2025 ShadowMario". It is a custom "please open-source" notice with no grant of patent/expression rights and no compatibility statement with Apache 2.0. The **code is derived from Apache-2.0 Psych 0.5.2h**, so anything that already exists upstream is fine to take from *Psych* directly (with Psych's attribution); the **small set of genuinely Joalor64-authored additions below should be re-implemented from their behaviour, not copied**, and attributed. That satisfies both the licence caution at the top of this file and the "port WITH THE APPROPRIATE CREDITS" rule.

**Headline:** Phoenix already contains ~95% of this repo. Every Lua shader callback, every gameplay changer, the options, HScript, achievements, Discord, dark mode, prompt, loading screen, crash handler, GameMode, mod packaging and the whole Psych 0.5.2h feature set are present — most of them in a larger form. The honest gap list is **three real features and a handful of small compatibility/comfort items**.

### 34.1. Real gaps worth carrying forward

- **[P2] Global colour-blind screen filter option.** Joalor64's `source/Colorblind.hx` (a `ColorMatrixFilter` applied to `FlxG.game` through `FlxG.game.setFilters`, gated on `ClientPrefs.shaders`) plus `ClientPrefs.colorBlindFilter` (`None`/`Deuteranopia`/`Protanopia`/`Tritanopia`) and an Options → Visuals and UI → "Colorblind Filter:" row (`options/OptionsSubState.hx:984`). Phoenix has the *matrices* already — `backend/ScreenFilters.hx:417-420` ships `protanopia`/`deuteranopia`/`tritanopia` and `psychlua/callbacks/FilterCallbacks.hx:119` exposes `addColorFilter(target, name, preset)` for cameras/`'global'` — and it has a *note-palette* preset (`ClientPrefs.noteColorblindPreset`, `options/VisualsUISubState.hx:241`). What is missing is the **in-engine Options row that applies one of those matrices to the whole screen**. Port: add `ClientPrefs.colorBlindFilter`, a Visuals and UI string option (same `Accessibility.COLORBLIND_PRESETS` list), and apply it through the existing `ScreenFilters`/`FlxG.game` path so Lua's `addColorFilter('global', …)` and the pref do not fight (one clears the other). This is the concrete instance of the still-open "in-engine Options menu toggle" for screen filters in §0. Default **None**, `shaders` gate, effort **S**. Credits: Joalor64; the file itself says "some code from Forever Engine, some from Flixel Demos".

- **[P2] Camera panning on sing.** Joalor64 adds `ClientPrefs.cameraPanning` + `panIntensity` and `PlayState.camPanRoutine()` (`source/PlayState.hx:1244-1268`), called from all four sing paths (`:3197`, `:3201`, `:3450`, `:3539`). On a sing animation it nudges a `moveCamTo` vector by `±40 * panIntensity * 240 / fps` in the sing direction (`:1260-1263`), but only when the singing character owns the section (`mustHitSection`, `:1252-1258`); `moveCamTo` is added to `camFollowPos` and decays back to 0 (`:2029-2040`). Phoenix has camera *follow* (`play/helpers/PlayStateCamera.hx:38,61-67`) and `cameraFxIntensity` for shakes/zooms, but **no per-sing directional nudge**. Do not confuse this with §25.2's `camMovement` toggle, which is about the *section target*; this is an additive impulse on top of whatever the camera is doing, so the two are independent prefs. Port: `cameraPanning` (default **on**, per the porting rule) + `panIntensity` slider (0.01–10, `%vX`), applied in the sing handlers and cleared on section change; must not fight `isCameraOnForcedPos` (`play/helpers/PlayStateEvents.hx:344-347`). Effort **S–M**. Credits: Joalor64.

- **[P3] Song-start banner with composer credits.** Joalor64's `PlayState` builds a HUD panel at song start — `bgThing` / `beforeSongText` ("Now Playing...") / `songText` (song name) / `whoDuntIt` ("Composer - …") at `source/PlayState.hx:773-800`, reads `credits/<song>.txt` for the composer line (`:775-778`), slides in at countdown start (`:1311-1319`) and slides out after ~3.7 s via `hideSongBanner()` (`:1469`). The code comment says "Credits to Unholywanderer04" (`:224`). Phoenix has no equivalent: its watermarks are commit/freshness, bot and platform only (`ClientPrefs.showCommitWatermark`, `botWatermark`, `platformWatermark`, `watermarkStyle`). Port as an optional HUD element (default on, toggle in Visuals and UI), reading `assets/credits/<song>.txt` with the mod-folder fallback (`Paths.mods(...)`), and keep it off `camOther`/on `camHUD` as in the original. Effort **S**. Credits: Unholywanderer04 (original), Joalor64 (integration).

### 34.2. Small / compatibility gaps

- **[P3] Combo-sprite position offset.** Joalor64's `ClientPrefs.comboOffset` has **6** entries; `[4]`/`[5]` shift the combo sprite itself (`source/PlayState.hx:3026-3027`) and `options/NoteOffsetState.hx` got two extra binding slots for it. Phoenix has **4** (`backend/ClientPrefs.hx:449`) — rating X/Y and combo-number X/Y — and `NoteOffsetState` has no combo sprite to drag. Effort **S**; only worth it if the combo popup position needs independent tuning from the digits.

- **[P3] `ALSOFT_CONF` audio-config handling.** Joalor64 ships `alsoft.ini` (renamed `alsoft.conf` off-Windows) and sets `ALSOFT_CONF` in `Main.__init__` (`source/Main.hx`) — Windows: next to the exe, mac: `Resources/alsoft.conf`, Linux: next to the exe — so OpenAL Soft picks up a shipped config. Phoenix has no `alsoft` file or env handling at all. Effort **S**. Worth it only if we ever ship an ALSOFT config (HRTF, resampler, buffer sizes) for low-end audio; otherwise skip.

- **[P3] HScript compatibility aliases.** Joalor64's `FunkinHScript` (Psych 0.5.2h) seeds `trace`, `importScript`, `stopScript`, `createThread`, `createSpriteGroup`, `createTypedGroup`, and `add`/`remove`/`insert`/`members`/`state` bound to `FlxG.state` (`source/FunkinHScript.hx:39,66,70,77,109,146-158`). Phoenix's HScript is a bigger, differently-shaped API — it has `debugPrint` (`psychlua/HScript.hx:720`), `addScript`/`addHScript`/`removeHScript`, `getRunningScripts`, `isRunning`, `callOnScripts`, `addHaxeLibrary`, `state`, `game` — but **not** `trace`, `importScript`, `stopScript`, `createThread` or the `FlxG.state` group helpers. For Psych-0.5.2h-era HScript mods, add thin aliases (`trace` → `debugPrint`; `importScript` → `addHScript` returning its locals; `stopScript` → `Function_Stop`/destroy; `createThread` → `sys.thread.Thread` on `sys`, plain call elsewhere; group helpers → `FlxG.state`). Effort **S**. Credits: MAJigsaw77 (Psych's base HScript code, per VS-Rob's own credits list).

- **[P3] Health-percentage text.** Joalor64 draws `healthTxt` = "Health: N%" above the health bar (`source/PlayState.hx:816-821`). Phoenix has the health bar and icon frames but no percentage label. Effort **S**; fold into the existing `healthBarStyle`/`timeBarStyle` family if added.

- **[P3] "Currently Playing: `<song>` | `<version>`" HUD line.** Joalor64's `versionTxt` (`source/PlayState.hx:840-843`). Phoenix has the commit/freshness watermark (`debug/CommitWatermark.hx`) but no song+version line. Effort **S**; could be another `watermarkStyle`.

- **[P3] `Alphabet` menu layout shapes.** Joalor64's `Alphabet` has `itemType` = `Classic` / `Vertical` / `C-Shape` / `D-Shape` (`source/Alphabet.hx:27,377`) — per-menu layout curves for the selector list. Phoenix's `Alphabet` is a different implementation (`objects/Alphabet.hx`, with `alphabetStyle`/`alphabetFont`/`reloadAll`) and has no layout shapes. Effort **M**; only worth it if a mod needs a non-linear menu, otherwise skip.

- **[P3] Letter-grade rating names.** Joalor64 hardcodes `ratingStuff` = `F-, F, D, C, B, A-, A, A+, S-, S, S+, SS-, SS, SS+, X-, X, X+, P` (`source/PlayState.hx:70-89`). Phoenix's `ratingStuff` is **quote-driven** (`data/ratingQuotes/<rateNameStuff>.txt`, `ClientPrefs.rateNameStuff`, `play/PlayState.hx:1035-1072`), so this is achievable today by dropping in a quotes file — no engine work. A built-in "Letter Grades" preset would still be a nice preset row. Cross-ref §0's "micd up ranking thing (S, A, etc..)". Effort **S**.

- **[P3] Video-skip prompt text.** Joalor64 shows `skipVidTxt` = "Press Z to skip cutscene!" (`source/PlayState.hx:1177-1186`). Phoenix has the skip *logic* (`play/CutsceneHandler.hx:93-107`, `objects/VideoSprite.hx`) but no on-screen hint. Effort **S**; only if cutscenes become common.

### 34.3. Checked and already covered — do not port

- **Every Lua shader callback** (`addChromaticAbberationEffect`, `addScanlineEffect`, `addGrainEffect`, `addTiltshiftEffect`, `addVCREffect`, `addGlitchEffect`, `addPulseEffect`, `addDistortionEffect`, `addInvertEffect`, `addGreyscaleEffect`/`addGrayscaleEffect`, `add3DEffect`, `addBloomEffect`, `clearEffects`) — Phoenix `psychlua/callbacks/EffectCallbacks.hx:47-228`, with more effects and per-camera registries. The diff of the whole Lua callback table shows VS-Rob's only unique name is `clearUnusedMemory`, which is upstream Psych and already in Phoenix (`backend/Paths.clearUnusedMemory`).
- **Whole Lua global/hook surface** — diffed `set()` globals (VS-Rob 68 vs Phoenix 170; the only VS-Rob-only names are `os`/`require`, which Phoenix deliberately sandboxes), `callOnLuas`/`setOnLuas` (VS-Rob's only unique events are `goodNoteHit`/`opponentNoteHit`, both fired at `play/helpers/PlayStateNotes.hx:456,625`), and the HScript table (Phoenix's is a superset).
- **Gameplay changers** — `scrolltype` (multiplicative/constant), `scrollspeed`, `songspeed`, `healthgain`, `healthloss`, `instakill`, `practice`, `botplay`, `opponentplay`, `randommode`, `flip`, `stairmode`, `wavemode`, `onekey` all exist in `backend/ClientPrefs.hx:407-435` and Phoenix adds `bothSides`, `opponentdrain`, `drainlevel`, `jacks`, `randomspeed`, `randomspeedmin/max`, `thetrollingever`; UI in `states/substates/GameplayChangersSubstate.hx`, opened from Freeplay and Story.
- **Options** — `shaders`, `ghostTapAnim`, `displayMilliseconds` (Phoenix `showMS` + `play/objects/MSText.hx`), hit windows (`sickWindow`/`goodWindow`/`badWindow`/`shitWindow` → Phoenix `AccuracyPresets` + `perfectWindow`), `safeFrames`, `ratingOffset`, `comboOffset` (first 4), `noteSplashes`, `hideHud`, `timeBarType`, `flashing`, `camZooms`, `scoreZoom`, `healthBarAlpha`, `showFPS`, `pauseMusic`, `ghostTapping`, `noReset`, `hitsoundVolume`, `controllerMode`, `downScroll`, `middleScroll`, `lowQuality`, `globalAntialiasing`, `framerate` — all present, most with more styles.
- **Global colour-blind matrices** — present as Lua presets (`backend/ScreenFilters.hx:417-420`); only the Options row is missing (§34.1).
- **HScript** — Phoenix's is a rewrite with `addHaxeLibrary`, `createCallback`/`createGlobalCallback`, `getSystemInfo`, `getRunningScripts`, `callOnScripts`, `hxTrace`, sandboxing and `Function_StopLua`/`Function_StopAll`; only the aliases in §34.2 are missing.
- **Achievements** — including `toastie` ("Toaster Gamer"), `ur_bad`, `ur_good`, `hype`, `two_keys`, `oversinging`, `friday_night_play` (`backend/Achievements.hx`), plus the menu and the `checkForAchievement` hooks.
- **Discord RPC** — `backend/DiscordClient.hx` + `changePresence` Lua callback.
- **Windows integration** — dark mode (`system/Window.hx`, applied from `Main.hx:268` via `matchSystemTheme`), message box (`backend/WindowNative.messageBox`, used by `Dialog.hx`), borderless/frame refresh.
- **`Prompt` substate** — `objects/Prompt.hx`; **`InputFormatter`** — `backend/InputFormatter.hx`.
- **Loading screen** — `states/LoadingState.hx` + `objects/LoadingPieDial` + `backend/LoadingDialLogic` (§0 DONE); Joalor64's `funkay`/`loadBar` is a skin.
- **Crash handling** — `backend/CrashHandler`, `CrashBundle`, `CrashBundleWriter`, `DiagnosticsReport`, `RecoveryBoot`.
- **Linux GameMode** — `Main.hx:24,68-85` (`hxgamemode.GamemodeClient`), same as Joalor64's `external/gamemode_client.h`.
- **Mod packaging / data files** — `mods/<folder>/pack.json`, `modsList.txt`, `weekList.txt`, `stageList.txt`, `characterList.txt`, `data/introText.txt`, `credits/` icons.
- **Menus** — story-menu tracklist (`states/StoryMenuState.hx:208`), pause exit choices incl. "Exit to Freeplay" (`states/substates/PauseSubState.hx:32`), freeplay missing-chart error (`states/FreeplayState.hx:84-85,259-262`), `ResetScoreSubState` with difficulty.
- **Camera zooms / screen filters / chainable effects / note RGB** — `backend/CameraEffects.hx`, `psychlua/callbacks/FilterCallbacks.hx`, `backend/ChainableEffects.hx`, `shaders/RGBPalette.hx` + `objects/NoteColors.hx` (Joalor64's `arrowRGB` + `ColorMask` is the same idea as Phoenix's RGB palette shader).
- **Video cutscenes + skip** — `objects/VideoSprite.hx`, `play/CutsceneHandler.hx`, `play/helpers/PlayStateCutscenes.hx`.
- **Score/rating plumbing** — `ratingFC` (`play/helpers/PlayStateRating.hx:435-453`), `fcStrings`, NPS + `maxNPS`, `smoothHealth`, `randomSpeedThing`, `showComboNum`, `comboFunction` — all present.
- **`Conductor.bpm` setter, `CoolUtil.colorFromString`/`precacheSound`/`browserLoad`, `Highscore.formatSong`, `MenuCharacter`/`Character` fallbacks, `PlayerSettings.reset`, `FlashingState` warning, `MusicBeatState` transitions, `FPS` counter, `Macros.getCommitId`, `WindowsAPI`-equivalent, `ColorMask`-equivalent** — all present in a newer form.

### 34.4. Deliberately excluded — mod-specific content (not engine)

Per the request, none of these are TODO material: the Rob week/songs/charts (`assets/songs/hello-friend`, `tutorial`, `assets/data/charts/*`), the `rob` character and BF reskin (`assets/characters/rob.json`, `assets/images/characters/*`), the `cityStage`/Skytopia stage (`assets/images/cityStage/*`, `assets/stages/*`), the title screen art and `FlickeringStar` (`assets/images/title/*`), the MSN-style main menu and freeplay art (`assets/images/mainmenu/bg_msn`, `assets/images/freeplay/slash`, `assets/images/menubackgrounds/*`), the credits list and `assets/images/credits/*`, the Discord client ID (`1384970716440563782`) and "VS Rob Rebooted" presence text, the achievement art (`assets/images/achievements/*`), `assets/credits/*.txt`, `mods/pack.json`'s placeholder name/colour, and the `assets/data/introText.txt` meme list. If any of that art is wanted it belongs in a mod/asset pack with the original artists credited, not in the engine.
